PART 13 — INSTRUCTIONS TO PASTE INTO ANY NEW CHAT
=======================================================================

"We are continuing development of LuckyDex. Read source/docs/LUCKYDEX_MASTER_RULES.txt from the latest uploaded source ZIP FIRST. Treat its [LOCKED] rules as binding and its [CHECK] items as unresolved, not automatically resolved by the code. Review the actual current version and card implementation before editing. Preserve saves, ownership, art, achievements, CPU logic, Test Lab, existing gameplay and all prior expansions. Do not use Codex unless I ask. When I add a new rule or we discover an exception, update this TXT, its changelog, the relevant card/engine tests and any set notes in the SAME patch. For new sets, cover every card, move, metadata, player/CPU deck-builder integration, Special Energy, targeting, cross-set rules and regression tests. Deliver a real, versioned changed-files patch with correct Termux installation instructions. Never mistake 'current code behaviour' for an approved design rule."


  [CURRENT] v0.69.209 adds EX Crystal Guardians (ex14), 100 numbered English cards, printed attacks/abilities, art URLs and ownership/set/player/CPU/Test Lab integration. Of those 100, 96 are marked battle-enabled/deck-eligible after supported-effect testing; four remain [CHECK]/PARTIAL and are visible but gated from live player and CPU decks: Delcatty ex #91, Swampert ex #98, Alakazam ★ #99, Celebi ★ #100. See docs/EX_CRYSTAL_GUARDIANS_v0.69.209_RULES_AUDIT.md for the complete 100-line card-by-card status and exact unresolved mechanics.
  [CURRENT] EX14 Crystal Beach temporarily restricts two-or-more-unit Special Energy to one Colorless Energy; Double Rainbow attaches only to Evolved non-ex and reduces damage; Cessation Crystal suppresses Poké-Powers and Poké-Bodies while attached to an Active legal Pokémon; Memory Berry allows compatible pre-evolution attacks and is discarded after attacking; Holon Circle ends an attack and discards itself. Specific damage, lock, move, supply/hand/discard, choice and timed triggers are implemented in shared rules plus the ex14 dispatcher.
  [LOCKED] For a new expansion, having a card's printed text, image and an executable `neoRule` name does NOT justify battleEnabled. Nonfunctional copied attacks, KO-replacement effects, discard routing or player-selected targeting must stay PARTIAL until meaningful state-outcome tests pass. Maintain metadata for Energy, tools, statuses, triggers, Bench synergy, evolution and conditional damage in the same pass.
  [CHECK]  v0.69.209 Android portrait UI/touch, save migrations and long self-play CPU strategy are unverified. Automated tests verify the 96-card battle-enabled subset and selected affected engine behaviour, not an exhaustive combinatorial proof. App versionCode 7189; v0.69.209. Existing fully historical test failures remain outside this new audit.

=======================================================================
  [CURRENT] v0.69.213 enables all 101 EX Dragon Frontiers (ex15) cards after completing the 19 previously gated effects and their outcome-level regression checks. Printed delta identity, choice timing, Prize flips, copied attacks, Energy movements, cross-board counters, pre-damage switching and delayed effects have targeted tests in tests/v07213-ex-dragon-frontiers-completion.test.cjs; the updated ledger is docs/EX_DRAGON_FRONTIERS_v0.69.213_COMPLETION_AUDIT.md. The set stays compatible with earlier Delta Species/Holon sets, EX14 Crystal Beach and the separate Classic Basic Energy Zone.
  [LOCKED] C-EX15-OPTIONAL-008: A printed "may" Power or attack effect offers an accept/decline decision BEFORE moving, revealing, discarding or attaching cards; declining leaves all zones unchanged. Multi-step effects must preserve their originally chosen object and cannot silently choose a default recipient. If a recovery effect specifies "1 of your Pokémon", the user/CPU can select an eligible Active or Bench target.
  [LOCKED] C-EX15-DAMAGE-009: A "before doing damage" switch resolves BEFORE Weakness/Resistance, not as post-attack repositioning. Damage modifiers apply in their printed order, especially against Weakness; placing damage counters does not become attack damage and bypasses Weakness/Resistance. Attacks that deal damage to a chosen Bench target must emit one Bench hit event per actual hit.
  [LOCKED] C-EX15-COPY-010: Attack-copy choices must respect the source restrictions, copied attack's original Energy requirements when specified, other printed costs and target/result behaviour; recursive copies must be bounded rather than trapping either player or CPU. Card lookup/search must account for Holon Veil's dynamic delta identity during the live battle. No unsupported generic "special effect" fallback can count as implementing a card.
  [LOCKED] C-EX15-ENERGY-011: A card that discards one Energy and recovers a DIFFERENT Energy cannot immediately recover the same physical card. When discard pools aggregate Basic Energy by type, preserve how many cards of that type existed before the discard to enforce distinct physical identity. Cross-set Holon Energy reprints share their activation rules and legal owner limits.
  [CHECK] The full relevant automated test run is 167/167 (v07205–v07213 selected suites) and does not substitute for Android touch QA, extensive long-match AI decisions, or exhaustively simulating every pairwise card interaction. Four earlier EX Crystal Guardians cards remain gated (ex14-91, ex14-98, ex14-99, ex14-100). No Pocket rules intentionally changed.

v0.69.214 SHARED MECHANICS AND USER-APPROVED UI REFINEMENTS
------------------------------------------------------------
C-PRIZE-CHOICE-001 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Classic Prize awards require the
claiming human player to choose a specific Prize POSITION after a knockout,
not take the next/first card by default. Hidden Prize identities must remain
secret in battle actions, previews, labels and screen-reader text. Cards
previously revealed by an effect must retain face-up identity and artwork.
Each two-Prize ex knockout permits TWO successive choices from the then-
remaining pile. KO/promotion, end-turn and victory resolution must resume only
after all applicable choices. CPU chooses without reading hidden identities.
Pocket point-scoring is unchanged.

C-PRIZE-UI-002 [LOCKED] In a Classic battle, tapping your Prize count opens a
preview of all remaining positions as face-down cards, except those already
revealed by a card effect. After a KO the same choices are actionable. New
reveals (e.g. Island Hermit) must not name an unrevealed Prize in the OPTION
prompt before the choice is committed. A face-up card remains visible until it
is drawn, exchanged or hidden by an explicit printed effect. Preserve the
existing chosen sleeve artwork on hidden positions. Enforce Classic hand cap
after collecting, without deleting cards.

C-DECK-RETREAT-008 [LOCKED] A passive Body such as EX Dragon Frontiers
Swellow delta Extra Wing is Stage 2 Pokémon-ex *retreat support*, not generic
Fire damage synergy. When eligible and Energy-compatible, the automatic
builder should pair its complete Taillow->Swellow line with a COMPLETE Stage 2
Pokémon-ex evolution line and vice versa; never leave an orphan Stage 2 or
invent a third Basic Energy type. Such support is optional for manual decks.

C-CPU-DOZING-009 [LOCKED] Snorlax delta Dozing is an OPTIONAL Poké-Power.
The CPU should use it when active, damaged enough to heal and legal, then
select the healing choice rather than the initial decline option. The
printed Asleep consequence and Bedhead between-turn trigger remain intact;
never turn Dozing into a forced auto-trigger for the human.

C-ENERGY-HAND-017 [LOCKED] Latias ex delta Fellow Boost attaches an actual
Basic Energy CARD FROM HAND and ends the player's turn. Do not consume the
next Basic Energy Zone card directly. The builder should reserve legal
physical-hand Basic retrieval (Mr. Stone's Project where present) so the Power
can function. Its action must not appear without a hand Basic card; inspection
must explain this restriction. Respect once-per-turn and legal target choices.

C-LAB-AUTO-003 [LOCKED] Test Lab Classic auto-builder preferences (selected
sets, attack Energy types, theme, rarity, Basic-only, random/comparison
options) persist independently from normal Classic and CPU opponent settings.
Saving a Test Lab deck preference cannot rewrite owned normal deck data,
rewards, collection, or normal opponent settings. Persist and restore across
Test Lab exit/re-entry, including previous saved schema migration.

C-UI-CARD-004 [LOCKED] In choice menus with recognisable full card artwork,
do not repeat the card name in large duplicate text blocks below the image.
Retain screen-reader alt/ARIA labels, readable prompts, and full text when
there is NO suitable artwork. Display printed Poké-Body versus Poké-Power
identity accurately, rather than labelling every legacy ability a Power.

[CHECK] This v0.69.214 pass changes global Classic KO/Prize timing; extensive
third-party mobile touchscreen and exotic simultaneous KO effects are NOT
fully verified. Historical manual-vs-current Classic board-size conflict
remains unresolved. EX Crystal Guardians' four prior PARTIAL cards remain
gated. See docs/LUCKYDEX_069214_CLASSIC_QOL_AUDIT.md for tests and caveats.

v0.69.215 EX DRAGON FRONTIERS — STAGE 2 EX / DECK-SYNERGY AUDIT
------------------------------------------------------------------
C-DECK-STAGE2-EX-010 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] A deck's automatic selection of a Pokémon whose main Bench Body or Power serves Stage 2 Pokémon-ex must include a COMPLETE, owned, legal Stage 2 Pokémon-ex evolution family when available in the chosen sets. Do not include Xatu δ solely as a weak attacker without a beneficiary or put a Stage 2 ex in a deck without its evolving Basic/Stage 1. Reverse synergy is also important: a Stage 2 ex attacker should favour Xatu δ's attack boost and Swellow δ's retreat reduction when their lines are legal and available. Treat Altaria ex δ's Extra Boost as hand-only Basic Energy acceleration; only include that support with a real way to get physical Basic Energy into hand, not the infinite Basic Energy Zone. The first priority in automatic Energy planning is paying for the Stage 2 ex ATTACKER, not forcing an off-type Bench support attack. Respect user-set type filters, allowed sets, ownership, 4-copy names, Pokemon Star restrictions and 30-card deck size. Special-purpose support can have a nonpayable off-type attack if its passive effect is independently useful, but don't incorrectly claim the off-type Energy is available. This is a general rule for future stages and cross-set engines, not just Xatu.

C-EX15-DAMAGE-012 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Xatu δ Extra Feather supplies +10 attack damage for EACH in-play, unsuppressed allied Xatu δ benefiting a Stage 2 Pokémon-ex, on both player and CPU boards, applied before Weakness/Resistance; do not collapse two separate Bodies into one Boolean bonus. Respect Power/Body suppression and the printed qualifying stage/ex requirement.

C-EX15-META-013 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Attack-effect metadata must identify all inflicted Special Conditions, including combined effects and target aliases such as “that Pokémon,” without falsely treating self-inflicted Sleep as an attack against the opponent. For EX15: Arbok δ has Burn AND Poison; Vibrava δ has Confusion. Use the printed targets for deckbuilding and CPU evaluations, not an overly narrow regex.

C-DECK-CONDITIONAL-011 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] If a featured Pokémon's printed damage, secondary effect or Body uses attached optional Energy of another type, allocate the conditional trigger type to automatic Basic Zone selection where feasible, after mandatory attack types and user-locked filter constraints. EX15 Typhlosion δ's Burning Ball needs Psychic to attack but Fire to reliably enable its printed Burn condition. Do not apply this secondary Energy automatically to unrelated attackers. Pokémon requiring Tools as a condition of an attack or bonus must get owned, legal physical Tools before generic Trainer fillers when possible. A Tool's printed effect must still be independently legal and useful.

C-CPU-EX15-014 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Do not advertise or have CPU attempt Altaria ex δ Extra Boost unless the side ACTUALLY holds at least one physical Basic Energy card AND controls a Stage 2 Pokémon-ex recipient; never pretend the Basic Zone is a physical hand card. Both sides must use identical preconditions and legal choices. Preserve other hand-only Power/Supporter selection constraints.

[CHECK] v0.69.215 code and automated tests cover 101/101 EX15 identities, 83/83 focused legal owned-deck constructions, selected explicit battle state outcomes, all printed EX15 attack action smoke calls with supplied Energy, and seeded CPU deck construction. This does NOT mean every card's effect was independently re-proven in every matchup, nor that long CPU self-play or Android touch UI has been certified. Old EX2/EX3 single-type deck coherence tests fail identically on unmodified v0.69.214 baseline; track as unrelated legacy bugs, not new EX15 regressions. EX14's four PARTIAL cards remain unchanged. See docs/EX_DRAGON_FRONTIERS_v0.69.215_FULL_PASS.md for per-card ledger and precise test scope.


v0.69.216 CLASSIC DECK-BUILDER FOLLOW-UP — CONDITIONAL PARTNERS / CHOICE ART
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
C-DECK-PARTNER-015 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] A 'preferred partner' in card
metadata is only a recommendation. It MUST NOT introduce an off-Energy
attacking evolution family in a single-type auto-deck unless its own attacks
are payable by the final Energy Zone, or a verified PRINTED in-play dependency
makes it independently useful as a non-attacking Bench support. In particular
EX15 Feraligatr δ Lightning cannot add an unpayable Fighting Meganium δ line
just because the metadata lists Meganium as a preferred partner. In-play
printed Regi-style requirements may intentionally include an off-type Bench
Pokemon, but ordinary editorial partner suggestions are not that exception.
Preserve optional mixed-type support when the player enables it and actual
mandatory multi-type attack costs on the featured attacker.

C-DECK-SUPPORT-016 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Stage-2-ex-only support Bodies/Powers
must have a complete, eligible Stage 2 Pokémon-ex beneficiary family before
auto-selection. Apply the rule at BOTH paths: the support Pokémon as the
selected main/focus or partner, AND a randomly selected earlier Basic which
auto-evolves into the support Pokémon. If the restricted type/set pool has no
matching Stage 2 ex, skip that random core and any inert support partner; an
explicit focus should explain why no viable deck can be built rather than
silently returning a useless Xatu, Swellow or Altaria ex line. A Pokémon Star
such as Charizard ★ δ is BASIC, not a Stage 2 Pokémon-ex, and does not qualify.
Do not violate the user's Energy filter just to activate an optional support.

C-UI-CHOICE-005 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] When a choice tile has working full card
art, show the art WITHOUT a second text-placeholder rectangle. Ensure CSS
[hidden] wins over display:grid/block rules for both fallback and image; if
the image fails to load, hide it and unhide the textual fallback. Maintain
ARIA labels and genuine text-only options. Applies to both player/CPU choice
surfaces using the shared component; no need to duplicate printed card text.

[CHECK] EX15 v216 fixes verified by deterministic Card/CPU builder tests and
static CSS fallback assertions; Android rendering and prolonged matches remain
to be tested on a device. The older EX2/EX3 type/cohesion suites still have
three pre-existing failures (same results as unchanged v214/v215 baseline), not
claimed fixed by this EX15 issue pass. See
LUCKYDEX_069216_CLASSIC_BUILDER_CHOICE_FIXES.md.

v0.69.218 EX POWER KEEPERS STAGE 2 — TARGET CHOICES AND ENERGY REPRINTS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
C-EX16-ENERGY-001 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] EX-era printed Darkness Energy,
Metal Energy and Multi Energy reprints must reuse the matching live engine
rules, not just copy printed Energy text into the card catalogue. Darkness
+10 applies to attacks by Darkness/Dark-name Pokémon before Weakness;
Metal damage reduction applies only when attached to an actual Metal Pokémon
(after Weakness/Resistance); Multi provides one any-type unit unless another
Special Energy is attached, in which case it provides one Colorless. No extra
Energy unit or unrelated Pokémon buff is created. EX16 exact IDs: 87/88/89.

C-EX16-CHOICE-002 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] A choice of an opposing target is
owned by the side named by the printed card, not automatically by the target
owner. Energy Removal 2 (ex16-74): flip first, then on heads let the TRAINER
OWNER choose opposing Active or Bench and subsequently a specific attached
Basic or Special Energy card; the selected card reaches the correct discard
ledger. Mawile ex16-17 Pull Away: when the defending player has 5+ cards,
THE DEFENDER chooses which cards to discard, one at a time until four remain;
next-turn automatic draw follows normal battle timing.

[CHECK] v218 adds 15 newly battle-enabled cards over the staged v217 release,
not a claim that all Power Keepers cards are complete. Retain gating for the
remaining 38 (Stadiums, Fossils, evolution-triggered Powers and Pokémon-ex).
Avoid blindly using Cacturne's Poison Structure unless Sidney's Stadium is
actually in play; the Stadium itself remains gated for decks in this build.
Automated node suites: tests/v07200 through v07218, with the exact final
pass count recorded in EX_POWER_KEEPERS_v0.69.218_STAGE2_AUDIT.md. Android
choice overlays and CPU match duration still need device QA.

v0.69.219 EX POWER KEEPERS STAGE 3 — SHARED STADIUMS AND REAL CHOICES
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
C-EX16-STADIUM-003 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] All five EX Power Keepers
Stadiums are two-sided continuous effects, active only while that Stadium
is in play: Battle Frontier suppresses Poké-Powers AND Poké-Bodies on
evolved Colorless/Darkness/Metal Pokémon; Drake’s Stadium reduces incoming
attack damage to Active Colorless Pokémon by 10 AFTER Weakness/Resistance;
Glacia’s Stadium removes Weakness only from non-ex Water Pokémon; Phoebe’s
Stadium reduces Psychic Pokémon retreat Energy by two Colorless; Sidney’s
Stadium prevents Sleep/Confusion/Paralysis on Darkness Pokémon and clears
those conditions on entering play. Altaria #2’s Synergy Effect heals 10 at
the between-turn checkpoint only while Drake’s Stadium is present and its
Body is not suppressed.

C-EX16-ENERGY-004 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Energy Recycle System lets the
owner choose EITHER exactly 1 discarded Basic Energy to HAND or exactly
3 discarded Basic Energy into the real PHYSICAL draw deck (when available).
Find eligible Basic Energy in both the separate discard ledger AND any
actual card discard; make each recovered type/card a real selection. Do NOT
send the three-card mode to the Energy Zone. Energy Switch must choose an
attached BASIC Energy unit on a source Pokémon and a DIFFERENT recipient;
it must not silently consume/move Special Energy.

C-EX16-SEARCH-005 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Scott searches for up to three
real, eligible Supporter/Stadium cards in any mix, and the player can stop
early. Steven’s Advice offers a genuine numeric draw choice up to the
number of opposing Pokémon in play, capped by the current hand limit; it
is not playable when the owner holds seven or more cards including Steven’s.
Pichu’s Cry for Help searches only Lightning non-ex Pokémon. Baby Evolution
chooses a real matching evolution from hand and removes all damage counters.

[CHECK] EX16 Altaria Surprise currently uses a seeded BLIND random opposing
hand-card selection, which correctly avoids exposing hidden choices but is
not equivalent to user-selectable facedown position; improve the choice UI
before claiming perfect printed-choice fidelity. The automated tests verify
persistent state, not live Android touch selection. Maintain the 24-card
gate and continue through remaining Fossils, Powers and ex/Star triggers.

v0.69.220 EX POWER KEEPERS COMPLETION — PRINTED CHOICES & FOSSILS
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
C-EX16-POWER-006 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] An ability that activates only when
played from hand to evolve (Cradily Evolutionary Call, Walrein ex Chilling
Breath) MUST NOT trigger on deck/effect evolution; Pokémon ★ and Absol ex
Bench-entry abilities similarly require a genuine hand-to-Bench action.
Resolve mandatory and optional choices through owner-correct battle-choice
prompts and the existing CPU/Test Lab mechanism. A KO-triggered Power such
as Lanturn Energy Grounding must run BEFORE the knocked-out Pokémon's
attached Basic Energy reaches the discard ledger, moving a selected eligible
unit directly to its printed recipient without creating fresh Energy.

C-EX16-FOSSIL-007 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Claw Fossil ex16-84, Mysterious
Fossil ex16-85 and Root Fossil ex16-86 act as Colorless Pokémon on Bench
but are still physical Trainer cards; they have no attacks and cannot
retreat or receive Special Conditions. Knockouts discard them but award NO
Prize card and do NOT increment knockout counters. Owner may discard a
Fossil from play on own turn before attacking. Their passive reactions
and evolution lines must keep using real battle slots and source IDs.

C-EX16-DAMAGE-008 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Repeated targeted hits, triggered
Bench counters and reactive attack damage must use the printed order of
choices, prevention and type rules. Armaldo Rock Blast chooses one valid
opposing target per discarded Fighting Energy card; Flygon ex Psychic
Protector discards 0–4 real hand cards chosen by the defender and reduces
incoming damage *after applicable attack calculation*; Absol ex Psychic
Pulse hits only opposing Bench Pokémon already carrying damage counters;
Lanturn Lightning Strike is 50 without discard or 90 after discarding
all Lightning Energy (not 40/90). Printed immunity and optional discards
must remain legal on both player and CPU turns.

[CHECK] v220 is a complete source-level EX16 card-enable pass, 108/108; this
is NOT an exhaustive print-image proof nor Android device test. All 344
selected v072xx automated EX-era/regression checks passed. The pre-existing
Altaria Surprise facedown-selection limitation remains pending. Detailed
card audit and device QA matrix: docs/EX_POWER_KEEPERS_v0.69.220_FULL_PASS.md.

v0.69.221 EX POWER KEEPERS POSTRELEASE AUDIT — PRINT FIDELITY AND CHOICES
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
C-EX16-PRINT-009 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] Poison Ring prohibits RETREAT only;
never reuse a shared lock that ALSO disallows attacks. A chosen spread hit
(Rock Blast) is attack damage and must respect the chosen defender's
protection, damage reduction and Stadium modifiers, while observing the
printed exception to Weakness/Resistance. Copy attacks waive only Energy
cost where printed; their other attack-use costs still execute, including
physical deck cards discarded by Volcanic Flame.

C-EX16-OPTIONAL-010 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] 'You may' Bench/evolution Powers
(Absol ex Cursed Eyes, Walrein ex Chilling Breath and Eeveelution Star
Crimson/Yellow/Blue Ray) require an actual Use/Decline choice. A declined
Power MUST change nothing. The CPU must consider each Star Power's impact
on both visible Active Pokémon, and may not peek at the rival's private hand.
Altaria Surprise must offer facedown opponent-hand POSITIONS, NOT card
names/art; the chosen position then returns its card to deck and shuffles.
Sableye Down Draw respects the converted Classic 8-card hand limit.

C-EX16-BUILDER-011 [LOCKED, 12 Nov 2026] For EX Power Keepers-ONLY builds,
explicit single-type filters may not be silently broadened to include a
second required attack type on Flygon ex, Salamence ex or their full lines.
A mixed-type deck needs an explicit compatible selection. Preserve earlier
sets' documented mixed evolution-family conversions as a separate rule;
never break previous Holon/Kingdra/Deoxys expectations merely to change EX16.
Status metadata must include Cacturne Poison Structure (power-originated
Poison) and Kirlia Dream Eater's Sleep payoff. Magneton ex16-16 focus under
a one-set filter remains restricted because a later Magnezone exists outside
that selected pool; do not silently accept an incomplete focused family.

[CHECK] EX16 v221 108/108 enabled; 128 attacks execute in a deterministic
smoke state; 80/81 Pokémon produce legal EX16-only focused auto decks,
with only the documented Magneton focus exception; 64 seeded one-type
builds did not add unselected Energy; nine CPU archetype decks tested.
The selected 357/357 v072xx Node tests passed. These verify a finite
representative matrix, NOT every printed state or Android touchscreen QA.
See docs/EX_POWER_KEEPERS_v0.69.221_FULL_PASS.md for 108-card ledger.

10.14N [LOCKED] C-DP1-TURN-022 / C-DP1-AUDIT-023 — D&P ATTACK DURATION AND ALL-ACTION GATES (v2.23)
  • If printed text says a Pokémon "can't attack during YOUR next turn", the lock MUST target the ATTACKER for nextOwnTurn, not opponentTurn and not only the named attack. Legal-action checks and CPU attack selection must reject every move on that next turn; the lock expires after that turn and is cleared when the Pokémon leaves the Active spot, evolves, or otherwise clears attack effects. Steelix Metal Slash, Onix Tunneling, Rhyperior Rock Wrecker, and Empoleon LV.X Hydro Impact are mandatory regression fixtures.
  • Printed attack protection "until the end of YOUR next turn" is not the same as "during your opponent's next turn". Staraptor Accelerative Dive on heads must persist through both the opponent's turn and the owner's following turn. It prevents damage from attacks by BOTH players, including Brave Heart's self-damage while the guard is active, and expires before the next opponent turn. Tails must do nothing.
  • Full-set verification is not achieved by effect-schema validation alone: each printed attack must be OFFERED by legalActions in a prepared payable/prerequisite-satisfying game state and must EXECUTE from both player and CPU seats with pending decisions resolved to completion. Exercise all Trainers likewise. Test next-turn locks by actually advancing turns, not just checking lock presence immediately after the attack. Test shields by dealing prevented damage, not merely inspecting expiry fields.
  • Preserve catalogue integrity: no printed text placeholders/no-op Trainers; each of the 130 D&P prints (107 Pokémon, 15 Trainers, 8 Basic Energy) has unique printed identity and supported effect. Two-sided attack/Trainer smoke is necessary, but not by itself sufficient for proving every possible printed outcome, random branch, AI strategy or Android animation. Document that distinction honestly.
  • Artwork-choice labels: simple card/Basic Energy art options should not repeat their own name or a generic BASIC ENERGY caption. Visible explanatory labels remain essential whenever outcomes differ, such as copied attacks, hand/Prize operations, voluntary discards, targets and attacks with the same image. Preserve accessible aria-label text even when redundant visible captions are hidden.
  • Keep older set regressions in the same suite; any failure must be classified against the preceding baseline and either fixed or reported, not masked by changing expectations to an incorrect card rule. Run a combined v072xx pass with concurrency limited when prior attempts stall.
=======================================================================

APPENDIX — v2.26 / v0.69.233 MYSTERIOUS TREASURES STAGE 3
=======================================================================
[CURRENT] Third incremental implementation stage, following v0.69.232: 106/124 collector entries are battle-enabled (96 Pokémon, 7 Trainers including Lake Boundary Stadium, 3 Special Energy prints); 18 remain gated. The preceding stage appendices are preserved as historical snapshots. This is not a full expansion completion release and is not yet the independently requested all-card audit.
[LOCKED] Benched/Active Poké-Bodies must affect both player and CPU via a common cost or damage adapter, not UI-only metadata: Ariados Sticky adds one retreat cost to every OTHER Pokémon; Mesprit Upper Material checks the Uxie/Azelf partnership and reduces the three Lake Guardians' retreat costs to zero; Mantine Jumbo Fin reduces allied Water retreat costs by two when Mantyke is beneath Mantine; Nidoqueen Mother Pheromone reduces one Colorless unit from qualified Nidoran family attacks. Respect printed exception and Body suppression.
[LOCKED] Weakness is a stadium-aware engine rule: Lake Boundary converts Weakness for both players to a full x2 calculation, rather than applying the normal Diamond & Pearl +10/+20/+30 amount. Resistance and damage-prevention order still apply correctly. Stadium replacement/removal must restore ordinary Weakness automatically.
[LOCKED] Aipom Throw Off removes all attached Tools from the chosen enemy target BEFORE dealing damage, including for Bench targets. Never turn an attack with a pre-damage printed effect into a post-damage optional action.
[LOCKED] Blissey Happy Chance offers an optional actual Basic Energy card from discard before damage; the Energy contributes to that hit's attached-Energy bonus. Kind Egg requires Happiny anywhere beneath Blissey, shuffles the selected 0..N cards from hand into the deck, then draws the same count (the shuffled cards may be redrawn).
[LOCKED] Unown M MAGNET must use actual active-only power timing, target Bench choices and coin outcome; its Hidden Power heads hits the defender for 30 and tails causes 30 self-side damage to the chosen own Pokémon instead of damage to the defender.
[LOCKED] Slaking Energetic Impulse is only available while Active and is used once per turn before attacking: heads increases Lazy Blow's BASE damage to 130 for that turn; tails blocks its attacks and retreat for that turn. Its effect expires if Slaking leaves the Active position, even if it returns during the same turn.
[LOCKED] Fossil Excavator is a Supporter with a real choice of qualified Fossil-named Trainer or matching Fossil-evolution card from the actual deck OR discard; never spawn cards from nowhere. Fossil-as-Basic Trainer cards Armor Fossil and Skull Fossil remain gated pending full play, KO and discard lifecycle implementation.

APPENDIX v2.34 — 14 NOVEMBER 2026 — v0.69.241 PRINT-BY-PRINT AUDIT + CARD-ART CHOICES
====================================================================================
[LOCKED] C-CHOICE-020: Whenever a card effect asks for an identifiable
physical Pokémon, Trainer, Stadium, Tool or Energy card, its choice data
MUST carry the exact `optionCardIds`/`choiceCardId` print identity so
all card-choice overlays render actual card art. Never rely on a label
alone if the physical card reference is available. Cards of the same
species from different expansions must retain the true print. Safe
unique-name fallback is permissible only for zones already known to
the chooser; NEVER expose face-down Prize identities, unknown opponent
hand contents or unrevealed opposing deck cards. Numeric decisions,
pass/fail, yes/no and true hidden options remain text or card backs.
Always distinguish physical Basic Energy prints from Energy Zone tokens.

[LOCKED] C-DP3-CHOICE-021: Furret's Baton Pass transfers ANY NUMBER of
its Basic and Special Energy cards after the optional Bench switch,
including zero, with an explicit Finish/Skip. Nidoking Poison Rub gives
the ATTACKER the option to switch the defending Pokemon BEFORE damage
and Poison. Golem Double Throw selects two DISTINCT enemy Pokemon where
two exist; it cannot strike the same Active twice.

[LOCKED] C-DAMAGE-022: Attacks that deal damage to a CHOSEN Active
Pokemon apply its printed Weakness, Resistance, attack modifiers,
and defensive Bodies/Tools unless explicitly overridden. For Benched
damage, apply the attack's printed exception (usually no Weakness or
Resistance). Distinguish damage COUNTERS from attack damage: counters
usually bypass the standard damage calculation. Verified Secret Wonders
examples: Jumpluff Cottonweed Punch, Cloyster Spine Missile, Golem
Double Throw and Xatu Energy Singe. Knock Outs and Prizes occur AFTER
all selected attack targets/effects have resolved.

[CURRENT] v0.69.241 source tests include fifteen new focused rule/UI
outcome checks in v07241-secret-wonders-independent-audit.test.cjs,
plus full dp3 and previous-set regressions. GitHub preflight runs this
new test file. `docs/SECRET_WONDERS_v0.69.241_PRINT_BY_PRINT_AUDIT.md`
tracks all 132 prints, their registered mechanics and remaining audit
risk tags. `generated/classic-audit-dp3.md` and JSON are reproducible.
These checks do NOT prove every printed ruling or certify a real phone.

APPENDIX v2.35 — 14 NOVEMBER 2026 — v0.69.242 BABY EVOLUTION DECK DEPENDENCIES
================================================================================
[LOCKED] C-DECK-BABY-001 — BABY EVOLUTION PAIRING IN AUTOMATED CLASSIC DECKS
  * Baby Evolution is a voluntary Basic-to-Basic Power from an actual card in hand.
    Its target (Smoochum -> Jynx, Pichu -> Pikachu, etc.) is not encoded by
    evolvesFrom and MUST be modelled as a separate deckbuilding dependency.
  * When auto-building or filling/repairing, never add an orphan Baby Evolution
    print without at least one owned, battle-ready compatible Basic target.
    Selection must obey enabled set filters and the chosen Energy plan.
  * When a Baby Evolution Pokémon is the featured/core card, reserve real
    copies of the target early, before generic support/Trainer filler. For a
    4-copy Baby core, prefer at least two target copies when available.
  * Do not add a third Basic Energy Zone type solely to support a Baby target.
    The target should have at least one attack payable by the chosen zone.
  * A saved draft containing a Baby with no partner can be repaired by adding
    a valid target; if that target is unowned/unavailable, exclude the Baby
    from the generated repair rather than silently preserve a dead combo.
  * Manual decks are not forbidden from including standalone Baby Pokémon:
    this is a recommendation/dependency rule for the auto-builder, not a new
    official gameplay restriction.
  * The shared Classic auto-builder serves both Test Lab and CPU opponents.
    Maintain a versioned regression test for Secret Wonders Smoochum/Jynx,
    older Baby Evolution lines, missing-target pools and Fill/repair.
[CURRENT] Source tests/v07242-classic-baby-evolution-deck-coherence.test.cjs
checks the above; Android Test Lab still needs device verification.
2026-11-14 | 2.34 -> 2.35 | v0.69.242 | Classic auto-builder Baby Evolution
dependency pairing for Psychic Smoochum/Jynx and cross-set equivalents.

=== VERSION 0.69.243 — GREAT ENCOUNTERS STAGED IMPLEMENTATION ===
- Expansion dp4 / Great Encounters has 106 historical card prints: 76 battle-enabled and 30 gated until their full printed actions can be resolved.
- A catalog entry alone does NOT mean it is battle-ready. Gated entries must be excluded from Test Lab auto-build, saved-deck repair and CPU-deck candidates.
- Maintain a numbered ready/gated ledger in docs/GREAT_ENCOUNTERS_v0.69.243_IMPLEMENTATION.md and independent auditor outputs in generated/classic-audit-dp4.{md,json}.
- Test each new mechanic with real game-state assertions: mandatory payments, optional choices, effect sequencing before prizes, hand/deck/discard ownership, bench selection, status conditions, actual damage (including Weakness/Resistance), evolution links and AI decision legality.
- Baby Evolution is not a conventional Stage 1 chain. Include a compatible real partner card (e.g. Igglybuff -> Jigglypuff) in any autogenerated deck.
- Active-only Powers must not be available from the Bench. The shared effect condition selfIsActive now supports this rule.
- If a printed card search/choice refers to a physical card, present a card ID/artwork choice. Textual labels are reserved for decisions, and concealed opponent cards must remain hidden except where the card reveals them.
- An auditor smoke-path skip is not a pass. Synthetic smoke completion also does not prove the entire printed effect or the Android choice UI.
- This release is not a full dp4 certification; its remaining 30 gated prints need a follow-up implementation and separate review.


=== VERSION 0.69.244 — GREAT ENCOUNTERS CONTINUATION AND TIMING ===
- Use the card catalog for the numbered source of truth. All 106 Great Encounters prints are registered; exactly 100 are battle-enabled, with Mawile #24, Unown G #57, Unown L #91, Darkrai LV.X #104, Dialga LV.X #105 and Palkia LV.X #106 gated pending verified mechanics.
- Do not enable a card until mandatory Energy/cost, physical card choices, reactive Powers/Bodies and player/CPU outcomes work. A smoke action completion alone is not printed-rule verification.
- Togekiss Serene Grace attaches Basic Energy from the ORIGINAL top 10 cards examined; cannot pull a newly exposed 11th card.
- Flame Kick: two Fire Energy discarded from Blaziken, choose a target, deal 80 (no Bench weakness/resistance). Twister: two flips, no effect on double-tails, choose one attached Defending Energy to discard per heads.
- Wailord Sleeping Pulse heals during between-turn check while Asleep. Weezing Toxic Virus adds 10 poison damage between turns to BOTH players' Poisoned Pokémon. Marvel Scale protects Milotic from opposing Pokémon LV.X attacks.
- Unown F Hidden Power must present a choice to flip or preserve guaranteed 10 damage; FAKE lets the opponent guess the hidden coin, not merely a predetermined CPU decision.
- Moon Skip on Cresselia LV.X takes an extra Prize only when its attack KOs the Defending Pokémon; preserve Prize selection sequence. Full Moon Dance can select source/target on either side.
- Physical card selection and opponent-visible choices are required wherever print says choose/search/discard. Hidden Prize cards must not be exposed without a printed effect permitting it.

=== VERSION 0.69.250 — FOSSIL EVOLUTION DEPENDENCIES IN CLASSIC AUTOBUILDER ===
[LOCKED] C-DECK-FOSSIL-DEPENDENCY-001 — Automatically generated Classic decks (competitive, random, CPU, Test Lab, and Fill/repair) must NEVER add an evolution Fossil card purely as a generic Trainer/filler Item. [LOCKED] When auto-filling or repairing a saved deck, drop orphaned Fossils. Do NOT remove Fossils from manual card selection or make otherwise legal manual decks invalid merely because of these strategic autobuild rules. Clefairy Doll, which shares the engine's playFossil implementation but is not an evolution Fossil, is exempt.

PLATINUM (pl1) — v0.69.265 BATTLE-ENGINE RULES
- Original 133 numbered and secret collector prints are the authoritative
  source, not placeholder labels. Regenerate with scripts/build-platinum.py
  from data/classic/pl1-print-source.json before producing an APK.
- Distinguish Poké-Powers from Poké-Bodies. Damage Bind disables Powers on
  damaged Pokémon; Time Crystal disables non-SP Bodies only; Power Spray may
  react to the opposing player's Power if at least 3 Pokémon SP are in play.
- A basic Energy Zone type is not the same as a physical Energy card in hand,
  deck, discard, or attached. All choices inspect and move real refs or update
  the appropriate zone ledger. Rainbow Energy supplies ONE flexible Energy.
- Pokémon SP are marked by the authentic printed SP subtype; Energy Gain only
  attaches to SP, and Poké Turn returns the selected SP and ALL attached cards.
- Broken-space Blow and Darkness Lost send a KO Pokémon and its full stack,
  Tools, special and Basic Energy to the owner's Lost Zone. Prize rewards and
  ordinary defeat flow are still resolved normally.
- LV.X is a Level-Up, not a normal evolution; its underlying previous-level
  attacks and Powers must remain available, and its Level-Up prerequisites
  must be retained when the deck builder selects an LV.X.
- Stadium effects such as Galactic HQ / Miasma Valley trigger when a Pokémon
  enters/evolves, not merely when the Stadium is played.
- Every optional attack or Power must present a readable, complete choice
  sequence; expert AI should make legal beneficial choices, not always Skip.
- Fossils should only appear in an autobuilt deck when their printed
  evolution partners are available and chosen; playable Fossils are Basic
  Pokémon while in play but Trainer cards in the deck/hand/discard.
- Run the entire GitHub APK workflow before declaring a build complete. The
  Stormfront deck-builder independent auditor must continue passing.

PLATINUM (pl1) — v0.69.266 CHECKLIST AUDIT / RELEASE GATE
- [LOCKED] Never accept a synthetic or partially recreated Platinum print archive, even if all 133 IDs are present. Match the exact original official Git blob SHA 594032984905672db5cc37fce0e9405bebb30f45 before a production build.
- [LOCKED] Run scripts/audit-platinum-checklist.py against freshly generated actual battle cards during Gradle APK preparation. Check every printed HP, type, stage/line, attack cost/damage/text, Power/Body, Trainer rule and enabled mapping; fail the build on any mismatch. A 133-row catalogue-only count is inadequate.
- [LOCKED] Vine Extract requires existing defender damage BEFORE attack, not damage caused by the current attack.
- [LOCKED] Cyrus's Conspiracy searches each of the three categories independently; no valid card in the first category must not cancel remaining categories.
- [LOCKED] Cacturne Poison Experiment chooses the physical attached Energy. Rainbow is ONE flexible Energy and must offer Grass (Poison) vs Darkness (Paralyze) rather than selecting Grass silently.
- [LOCKED] Golduck Water Slide optionally moves ALL attached Energy copies, then deals one 40 (or 60) attack hit before Weakness, Resistance and protection; never apply a second 20-damage hit.
- [LOCKED] Automated Platinum decks may not choose SP-only Tools without SP beneficiaries, Power Spray without at least three SP Pokémon, Level Max without LV.X or Memory Berry without a supported evolution.
- [RELEASE] The 221/221 attack smoke confirms no crash/stuck choice in representative battle states, not complete damage/target/CPU correctness. Preserve honest separation between authenticity, static mapping, live effect verification and phone QA.

=== VERSION 0.69.268 — RISING RIVALS (PL2) STAGE 2, 89 / 120 BATTLE-READY ===
2026-11-18 | The 120 authentic Rising Rivals collector prints remain available for collection and inspection. Battle/deck eligibility rises from 49 to 89; the other 31 have unverified mechanics and MUST remain gated. This is NOT a claim of full-set completion.
[SP ENERGY] SP Energy supplies exactly ONE flexible typed Energy only to a Pokémon SP; on a normal Pokémon it provides Colorless. Upper Energy provides two Colorless units only when its controller has more remaining Prize cards than the opponent and the recipient is not a Pokémon LV.X. Special Energy must retain its physical attachment identity.
[ATTACHMENT] Vespiquen E4 Royal Gain removes one damage counter on a Grass Energy card attached from the HAND, not from the Energy Zone. Flint's Willpower moves a real basic Energy card from hand onto a selected Pokémon SP and fires valid hand-attachment triggers. Bebe's Search returns one actual other hand card to the deck before searching for a chosen Pokémon. Bebe's cannot be played as the only card in hand.
[COMBAT] Nidoking's Giga Horn flips two coins before the hit and is entirely zero on two tails; Fling Away reduces its active attack damage when an opposing Bench exists, then chooses one Benched target for 30; Territoriality counters an actual attack on the own Active with exactly 20 damage, at most once even with multiple Nidoking. Beedrill Needle Shock does not reapply Conditions when the same attack was used by any own Pokémon on the preceding own turn. Vespiquen Leaf Needle flips once PER attached Grass Energy. Kecleon Colorful Body provides all printed Pokémon types, unless suppressed. Snorlax LV.X Exercise discards individually selected hand Energy and heals 10 per discarded card. Mismagius GL LV.X Darkness Magic places capped direct damage counters, not calculated attack damage subject to Weakness/Resistance. Alakazam E4 LV.X Damage Switch can transfer a chosen counter between two distinct Pokémon SP.
[CPU AND CHOICES] The player and CPU share the same legal card actions, explicit target/hand/discard choices and stage-by-stage completion. Do not silently resolve optional effects without considering valid targets. Do not accidentally double a single Energy card or multiply bench damage by Weakness. Gated prints never enter the auto deck pool.
[IMPORTANT GATE] Arcanine's Burn Out chooses whether to do 30 extra damage before applying Weakness. A post-damage generic bonus resolver is incorrect; Arcanine stays gated until a true pre-damage choice is supported. Never enable a card only to make its registration count higher.

BLACK & WHITE: EMERGING POWERS (BW2) — v0.69.298
============================================================
Canonical source: PokemonTCG/pokemon-tcg-data cards/en/bw2.json, pinned as
 data/classic/bw2-print-compact.json and verified by scripts/build-bw2.py.
98 distinct numbered prints: 91 Pokemon, 7 Trainers, including separate
full-art Thundurus #97 and Tornadus #98. All are available in set selection,
collection, Classic deck builder, CPU decks, and Test Lab through the common
registry. Regenerate assets and run tests in the APK build.

Mechanical rules to preserve in future sets:
- Modern Abilities and earlier Poke-Powers/Poke-Bodies are separate classes.
  Gothitelle (Magic Room) prevents only the opposing player's Items, only
  while Gothitelle is Active, unless the Ability itself is suppressed. It
  must not block a Supporter or Item played by Gothitelle's owner.
- Nurturing evolves a chosen owned Pokemon from an actual matching Evolution
  card in the deck, without first moving that card to hand; any ordinary
  evolution-on-turn restrictions are bypassed by this attack's explicit text.
- Helping Hand and Thundurus Charge must consume the selected physical basic
  Energy from the deck or the ordered Energy Zone; never invent attachments.
- Max Potion heals the selected Pokemon and moves *all* its attached Energy
  into the appropriate physical or Basic-Energy discard accounting.
- Great Ball reveals only the deck's current top seven; selecting a Pokemon
  places that physical card into hand and shuffles the rest back into deck.
  'Decline' is a real choice; unavailable non-Pokemon cards are not targets.
- Crushing Hammer, Deleting Glare, and Black Eyes target only Pokemon with
  Energy, and the player chooses which attached Energy is discarded. Black
  Eyes is optional and only usable with Krookodile Active.
- Power Whip, Feint Attack and other chosen-target attacks must present clear
  target choices and honor printed Weakness, Resistance and effect bypasses.
- Optional Superpower and Quick Tail Smash coin choices occur before damage
  matching, with recoil and tails gate applied exactly as printed.
- Shear, Energy Wheel, Hurricane, Tailwind, Charge Beam and Recycle move real
  cards or tracked Basic Energy units between correct battle zones. Special
  Energy is moved as a whole physical attachment, not as a free Basic Energy.
- Phat Sound uses the *same* three flips against every opposing Pokemon;
  active Weakness/Resistance applies but Bench Weakness/Resistance does not.
- Sacred Sword and Zap Cannon restrict only their named attack on the next
  own turn, not all attacks. Encore restricts all except the chosen move.
- Harden checks the final damage threshold (40 or less) after matchup math.
- Include these rules in Classic checks and never assume a schema-clean card
  is functionally correct without an executable scenario test.

  Physically discarded cards (including Special Energy and some Basic Energy

  sides[side].energySupply[] is FRESH Energy, NEVER discarded Energy.

  physical discard cards and the matching Basic Energy discard ledger,
  both through a validated, visible choice menu. Recovered Basic Energy
  decrements energyDiscard[type] EXACTLY ONCE and yields one real playable
  Basic Energy card in hand. No extra fresh Energy or copies may appear.

  dp2-48 Gabite (Gather Up), and pl2-33 Snorlax (Pick and Collect).

  remain confined to physical discard cards.

  are exhausted, or the Classic eight-card hand limit is reached. Never
  expose the opponent's discard pool or zero-count entries.

LUCKYDEX 0.69.301 — EMERGING POWERS DISCARD / ENERGY / HAND AUDIT
------------------------------------------------------------------
- Discard effects such as Emerging Powers Recycle use the owner's physical discard
  plus individually counted spent Basic Energy in the Classic Energy discard
  ledger. Recovered ledger Energy is removed from the ledger, instantiated as a
  physical era-appropriate Basic Energy card, and put on top of the deck. It
  must NEVER draw from the fresh ordered Energy Zone.
- A Trainer currently resolving its own effect may be present in the engine's
  discard[] for implementation purposes, but is not a legal candidate to
  retrieve with that same Trainer's effect. Earlier completed Trainers in
  discard are normal candidates. These choices work for human and CPU players.
- Any conditional attached-Energy attack text counts older Rainbow Energy
  prints (including energy encoded as Miracle) as providing the named type.
  Emerging Powers Simisage, Simisear, Simipour and Mandibuzz follow this rule.
- A delayed Trainer search decision, including Great Ball, must not exceed
  the Classic eight-card hand limit. If a choice becomes invalid because the
  hand is full, retain the card in its original deck and shuffle as required.
- Keep exact player choices for Superpower and Quick Tail Smash; losing the
  optional choice is a gameplay regression, even if damage calculation passes.
- Run tests/v07301-emerging-powers-deep-audit.test.cjs at every APK build.

LUCKYDEX 0.69.302 — BLACK & WHITE: NOBLE VICTORIES (BW3)
------------------------------------------------------------------
[CATALOG] Include all 102 distinct collector prints in Noble Victories, including
  97–100 full-art Pokémon, 101 full-art N, and 102 secret Meowth. Never collapse
  duplicate named prints into one attack record. All 153 attacks and 12 Abilities
  must preserve their own printed text and effect definitions.
[RESTORED] Tirtouga (#25) and Archen (#66) have the special printed Restored
  stage, NOT the Basic stage. They cannot be used in opening setup or played
  directly from hand as Basic Pokémon. Cover Fossil (#90) and Plume Fossil
  (#93) look only at the bottom SEVEN *physical* cards of the owner's deck,
  may reveal and Bench the matching Restored Pokémon, then shuffle the
  remaining cards back into the deck. The player may decline. Once in play,
  each Restored Pokémon can evolve normally into Carracosta or Archeops.
[ABILITIES] Black & White Abilities are NOT legacy Poké-Powers or Bodies.
  Leaf Tailor suppresses Weakness only on friendly Pokémon with Energy;
  Carracosta's Solid Rock flips on incoming attack damage and applies the
  50-point reduction AFTER Weakness/Resistance; Conkeldurr's Craftsmanship
  increases maximum HP by 20 per attached Fighting Energy; Hydreigon's Dark
  Aura turns all attached Basic/Special Energy into Darkness, preserving unit
  count; Archeops's Ancient Power blocks hand-played evolutions for BOTH
  players (including Rare Candy), not evolution caused by attacks/Abilities.
[REACTIONS] Jellicent Cursed Body confuses the ATTACKING Pokémon when its
  owner is Active and is damaged by an opponent's attack; Druddigon Rough
  Skin places 2 damage counters on the ATTACKING Pokémon under the same
  conditions. Both trigger before KO resolution, including a lethal attack.
  Rocky Helmet deals 20 damage to the attacker when the equipped Active is
  damaged. Eviolite reduces damage by 20 after matchup only when attached to
  a Basic Pokémon. Do not register these as inert, passive once-only effects.
[DISCARD] Eelektrik Dynamotor retrieves an actual Basic Lightning Energy
  from the physical discard pile OR the separate Basic Energy discard ledger,
  attaches it to an owner's Benched Pokémon, and is used once per turn. Never
  generate fresh Energy or offer a dead CPU action with no legal source/target.
  Volcarona Fiery Dance, Landorus Abundant Harvest and Bisharp Energy Stream
  similarly use an actual discarded Basic Energy card. Super Rod shuffles
  three selected Pokémon/Basic Energy from physical discard and/or the ledger
  back into the physical deck, decrementing the ledger for every recovered
  Basic Energy card. Trubbish Garbage Collection returns a real discard card
  to the deck top; the resolving effect cannot select its own current Item.
[CHOICES] Victini Stored Power moves all attached Energy to one CHOSEN own
  Benched Pokémon. Gigalith Core Cannon selects an opposing Pokémon and
  counts Fighting Energy attached to the attacker (including compatible
  Special Energy). Chandelure Cursed Shadow selects opposing Pokémon to
  receive exactly three damage counters in any legal distribution. N shuffles
  each player's hand back into their own deck, then draws for their own
  remaining Prizes, respecting LuckyDex's eight-card hand cap.
[COINS] Victini Victory Star offers its optional attack coin-flip reroll once
  per turn across copies; V-blast requires BOTH heads to deal damage; V-create
  requires five Pokémon on the owner's Bench for its printed attack; otherwise
  its damage is zero, even if the compact format rarely permits a five-Bench
  state. Never silently relax the printed condition in CPU attacks.
[ANALYSIS] Dynamic damage for Round, Net Force, Electrichain and Core Cannon
  must be computed BEFORE the Weakness/Resistance pass. Electrichain's extra
  damage requires a Lightning Pokémon on the Bench, not its Lightning-type
  attacker alone. Leaf Wallop adds 40 to its own attack on the next turn;
  Slashing Strike cannot use its *named attack* during the next owner turn.
[TEST] Run scripts/build-bw3.py and tests/v07302-noble-victories.test.cjs
  in the preBuild/asset-merge gate. Also run the full classic expansion audit
  with `node scripts/audit-classic-set.cjs --set bw3 --smoke`.
[INSTALL] Apply v0.69.302 ZIP over v0.69.301 in source/; git add/commit/push.

  the globally disputed Bench size. The auto-builder must avoid this dead

  than output an unusable 30-card deck. The long-term Classic 3-vs-5 Bench

[0.69.306 / Black & White: Dark Explorers]
* The 111 printed Dark Explorers cards (bw5-1..111) retain distinct print IDs,
  canonical attack/Ability text and 2-Prize Basic Pokémon-EX handling.
* Effects retrieving Basic Energy from discard (e.g., Grand Flame, Dark Patch,
  Charge Beam) must access physical discard and the genuine Basic Energy discard
  ledger; never create fresh energy from the live Energy Zone for these effects.
* Dark Patch can attach only discarded Basic Darkness Energy to a Benched Darkness
  Pokémon. Darkrai-EX's continuous Dark Cloak grants zero retreat cost to allied
  Pokémon with Darkness-providing Energy and respects Ability suppression.
* Old Amber Aerodactyl can put an owned Restored Aerodactyl onto the Bench from
  the bottom seven physical deck cards. The Pokémon cannot be benched from hand
  as an ordinary Basic; the deck builder must pair it with the Old Amber Item.
* Printed attack bonuses and coin conditions are resolved before applying
  Weakness and Resistance. Choices must name the exact Energy/Tool/target and
  make decline options explicit. Slurp Shakedown promotes its targeted opponent
  Bench Pokémon before damage is calculated against that newly Active target.
* Solar Revelation guards energy-bearing allied Pokémon against effects of
  opposing attacks (not damage); Ancient Scream and Dark Claw boost eligible
  damage; Scorching Scales changes opponent Burn checks; Slowbro Airhead gates
  attack actions at an even number of remaining Prize cards.
* Game retains its established 30-card physical deck, separate Basic Energy
  Zone and eight-card hand cap. Special Energy remains a physical deck card.
* Build-time checks: scripts/build-bw5.py then
  tests/v07306-dark-explorers.test.cjs before APK asset merge.

[0.69.307 / Dark Explorers Independent Audit]
* Discard-to-attachment searches MUST filter physical cards to Energy only;
  the separate Basic Energy discard ledger is also a legal source. Searches
  specifically requiring Basic Energy (e.g. Leafeon Energy Assist) exclude
  physical Special Energy; those permitting any Energy (e.g. Charge Beam) may
  attach physical Special Energy with its UID and card identity preserved.
  Never transform a Trainer or Pokemon in the discard into an Energy counter.
* Chosen attack targets MUST enter the ordinary attack damage pipeline.
  Raikou-EX Volt Bolt applies Weakness/Resistance to its Active target,
  ignores those modifiers on the Bench, and discards actual Lightning Energy
  after attack damage resolves. Both full-art and normal prints reuse this.
* Multi-target attacks that can choose the opposing Active (e.g. Kyogre-EX
  Dual Splash) apply normal Weakness/Resistance only to that Active hit;
  Benched hits ignore these modifiers. Preserve explicit choices and do not
  assume that all multi-target damage is Weakness-free.
* Cofagrigus Chuck must determine Tool-discard damage once, prior to type
  matchup; after the attack it discards only explicitly selected physical Tool
  cards and must never apply duplicate direct damage. Unique selection IDs
  prevent duplicate-count exploits.
* Item-play legality checks must honor mandatory costs/targets: Ultra Ball
  cannot be played without TWO other hand cards, Dark Patch needs discarded
  Basic Darkness and a Benched Darkness Pokemon, and Enhanced Hammer needs
  an actual opposing attached Special Energy card. These must not consume a
  card on a knowingly impossible action.
* v0.69.307 build gate: v07307-bw5-independent-audit.test.cjs executes after
  printed source generation plus the v07306 full-set tests. Android build and
  device verification remain separate requirements.


=== v0.69.308: BLACK & WHITE DRAGONS EXALTED (BW6) ===
- All 128 numbered prints are catalogued with pinned official card data; Dragon type is distinct from the Energy types required by printed attacks.
- Blend Energy GRPD (#117) and WLFM (#118) each count as exactly ONE unit, usable as Colorless or one of the four specified types. They never provide all four simultaneously. Special Energy is a physical 30-card-deck card.
- Rebirth is explicitly usable from the physical discard pile once per turn, only with an available Bench slot and three different discarded Basic Energy types. On heads, choose distinct discarded types and attach REAL discarded Energy, preserving the separate Basic Energy discard ledger. Never draw new Energy from the Energy Zone.
- Rayquaza-EX Dragon Burst offers an explicit choice to discard all Basic Fire or all Basic Lightning attached; damage is 60 times physical cards discarded, calculated before Weakness/Resistance. Do not silently select one.
- Ho-Oh-EX / Mew-EX / other Pokémon-EX give TWO selectable Prizes when knocked out; Shedinja Empty Shell awards none. Rescue Scarf returns only the KO Pokémon to hand, discarding its attachments.
- Mew-EX Versatile can use printed attacks of Pokémon in play on both sides, paying costs from Mew's own Energy. Garbotoxin disables Abilities (except Garbotoxin) while its Garbodor has a Tool, including Rebirth, and restores them when the Tool goes.
- Altaria Fight Song gives +20 pre-Weakness to Dragon attacks targeting Active; Bouffer reduces received attack damage by 20; Safeguard prevents attack damage/effects from Pokémon-EX; Giant Cape adds +20 maximum HP.
- Tool Scrapper must allow one or two legal attached Pokémon Tool selections, including both players; second choice may be declined. Physical cards return to the correct owner's discard pile.
- Attack choice, coin and target-resolution log events MUST finish before the Prize picker. Existing Classic 30-card deck/8-card hand/ordered Basic Energy Zone are unchanged.
- APK build gate: scripts/build-bw6.py, tests/v07308-dragons-exalted.test.cjs.

- Ninetales #19 Hexed Flame counts each distinct Special Condition currently affecting the Defending Pokémon, +50 each, before Weakness and Resistance; Burn is not the sole condition.
- Marowak #61 Vortex Chop adds 30 only if the Defending Pokémon has Resistance; Retreat Cost does not affect the move.
- Probopass #82 Magnetic Lines is optional and may move a REAL attached Special Energy card as well as a Basic Energy counter to the opponent's Bench; maintain its original UID, no minted replacement.
- Registeel-EX #81/#122 Triple Laser damages three distinct opposing Pokémon: the Active target receives Weakness/Resistance normally; Benched Pokémon ignore both.

2026-11-20 / v0.69.310 / BOUNDARIES CROSSED (bw7)
- Register all 153 numbered prints, retaining true printed 216 attacks and 21 Abilities. Printed EX variants retain unique card IDs; EX knockout awards two selectable Prizes.
- ACE SPEC cards are limited to ONE TOTAL card across an entire 30-card physical deck, regardless of different names or prints. Validate in both direct deck editing and CPU autobuild.
- Crystal Edge boosts only White Kyurem-EX, and Crystal Wall sets Black Kyurem-EX maximum HP to 300 only while attached. Removing the Tool restores natural HP without healing existing damage.
- Psychic Musharna Dream Waltz may be used while Asleep and adds 30 damage in that condition. Whimsicott Fluffy Tag boosts the selected new Active only during the next own turn, before Weakness/Resistance.
- Liepard Assist flips a coin and, if heads, lets the attacking player select and copy an attack on one of their Benched Pokemon. Never search for, consume, or auto-play a Supporter.
- Hugh makes the OPPONENT resolve hand size 5 first, and then the player; when over 5 the owning player selects physical cards to move to their DISCARD, not back to deck.
- Audino Busybody is an ACTUAL playable hand action. Reveal and discard Audino, heal 10 on Active, and remove exactly ONE chosen Special Condition (never all simultaneously). The hand action must be reachable in mobile UI.
- Dusknoir Sinister Hand moves real damage counters with explicit target selections. Draw In / Dragon Stream must consume existing discarded Basic Energy from the physical discard or tracked Basic Energy ledger, never create free Energy from the Zone.
- Town Map exposes actual current Prizes, without redrawing or replacing them. Optional attack costs and all Tool attachment effects preserve physical card identity and per-turn limits.
- Apply Weakness/Resistance to Active-target damage and not to Benched damage. One player choice must resolve visibly before a pending Prize selection interrupts the flow.
- Never promote a card to FULL solely because it passes a schema audit. Include direct action and printed-effect regression tests; audit static flags in a separate pass.

2026-11-20 / v0.69.312 / PLASMA STORM (BW8) INTEGRATION
- Plasma Storm follows Boundaries Crossed (BW7), has 138 collector-numbered English prints (including three secrets), and uses an immutable public PokemonTCG/pokemon-tcg-data source blob SHA 5ae56545ca722ef9ebfee78ae434ce416efa4277. To avoid altered card names/effects, the source must be fetched and Git-blob SHA verified once. The cached compact print file is generated under data/classic/bw8-print-compact.json and committed after `sh scripts/prepare-bw8.sh`.
- All 138 numbered prints must be registered; any attack, Ability or Trainer with unimplemented printed text MUST have battleEnabled=false and deckEligible=false. Never silently substitute noop for printed effects. Printed-effect resolution requires separate Android and manual audits, not just generated-schema coverage.
- Team Plasma is a card-specific tag, NOT a universal suffix on all Pokémon or Trainers. Plasma Energy (#127) is a PHYSICAL Special Energy from deck/hand/discard that provides one Colorless, and cannot be substituted with a basic Colorless Energy Zone counter. Colress Machine selects real Plasma Energy from the physical deck and attaches only to a Team Plasma Pokémon.
- Plasma Energy prerequisite attacks (Moltres-EX Power Flame, Articuno-EX Frost Prison, Zapdos-EX Powervolt, Lugia-EX Plasma Gale) check the attached physical card. Plasma Gale consumes an actual Plasma Energy card with its original UID. Lugia-EX Overflow earns ONE extra selectable Prize only for a KO caused by damage from its attack.
- Team Plasma synergy metadata must distinguish Team Plasma Pokémon, Trainers and Special Energy. Snorlax Teampact counts only Team Plasma Pokémon in play, including itself. Klinklang Plasma Steel prevents damage from Pokémon-EX attacks against the owner's Metal Pokémon; Magnezone Dual Brains permits TWO Supporters that turn, without leaving the extra Supporter unlocked on following turns.
- Hypnotoxic Laser sets Poisoned, and only heads additionally Asleep. Virbank City Gym adds exactly 20 poison damage per between-turn checkup (including stronger poison variants). Crobat Ultra-Toxic Fang uses 40 poison damage as its normal tick, then Stadium bonuses. These effects are NOT extra direct attack hits.
- Lucario Dual Armor gains Metal type with Basic or Metal-providing Special Energy; its deck-building metadata must include a conditional Metal energy preference. Victory Piece lets ONLY Victini-EX use attacks without paying their Energy costs. Eviolite reduces Basic Pokémon attack damage by 20 after Weakness/Resistance, including damage on the Bench if applicable.
- Optional attacks and multi-target effects must show clear choices (e.g. Ludicolo Groovy Dance, Braviary Wild Edge); preserve the selected option across effect-choice continuations. Do not duplicate Active damage when an attack also hits the Bench (Weezing Smogbank / Cubchoo Hail). For Power Flame and other Special Energy checks, use physical attachment identity, not mere Basic Energy counters.
- Trainer items such as Dowsing Machine must pay two actual discard-from-hand costs, exclude the still-resolving card as its own recovery target, and retrieve one selected Trainer from the real discard. Keep source/target selection explicit for Scramble Switch and Escape Rope.
- Bright Veil selective immunity (Togekiss #104) is intentionally gated until an exact per-Item/per-target implementation is available; do not allow a wrong no-op passive. Other missing printed effects discovered by the generator must similarly be gated until fixed.
- Local verification: 70 existing BW6/BW7 regression tests plus nine independent Plasma mechanic tests passed against a temporary registry fixture. Complete 138-card catalogue, CPU autobuild, online first-fetch, Android APK and device validation require the real immutable source to be fetched by prepare-bw8.sh and are NOT claimed as tested yet.
- Always run `sh scripts/prepare-bw8.sh` BEFORE committing/updating this patch; it saves the pinned authentic source, outputs the generated JS and coverage JSON, and runs v07312 tests. GitHub build gates regenerate BW8 first, run previous tests only after it exists, and run both BW8 catalog/CPU and Plasma mechanics tests before packaging assets.

2026-11-20 / v0.69.313 / PLASMA STORM BW8 MASTER-CHECKLIST AUDIT
- After generating the exact pinned 138-print BW8 source, confirm 137 battle-enabled cards, BW8 #104 Togekiss gated, all other cards catalogued with printed attack and Ability identity. A clean schema report is not proof that every attack executes correctly: the static audit currently reports 78 review flags and 270 skipped generic smoke actions (144/416 exercised). These require separate scenario coverage and on-device review.
- Team Plasma Grunt can discard ANOTHER physical Team Plasma Grunt with the same card ID (distinct UID). A cost-payable check MUST exclude only the played card's hand index/UID, NOT all cards with its card ID.
- Dowsing Machine may be played with two other hand cards if one is a Trainer, even when the physical discard currently has no Trainer: the printed two-card discard cost precedes choosing a Trainer from discard. Cannot recover its own still-resolving UID. All selections retain exact source UID.
- Donphan Wreck discards the physical Stadium to the Stadium OWNER's discard, never always the attacking side's discard. Preserve the original Stadium ref and UID.
- Cubchoo Hail and Weezing Smogbank damage the opposing Bench using standard attack damage protections, including attack guards and Eviolite on Basic Pokémon. Their separate Active hit is handled by normal attack resolution, not duplicated in the Bench effect. Classic generic benchDamage now includes Basic Eviolite reduction in the same after-modifiers stage.
- Clefable Moon Guidance must obey normal evolution timing: no evolving a Pokémon played/evolved this turn, no evolving twice this turn; if successful preserve the physical stack, record playedOnSideTurn/evolvedOnTurn and trigger onEvolved events. Target and evolution card are player choices when multiple options exist.
- Scramble Switch's Energy movement is optional: the explicit Done menu option exits without reopening the same chooser. Real moved Basic and Special Energy remain attached to the correct Pokémon without creating phantom copies.
- CPU-deck roles are semantic, not keyword matches. The word 'attached' is NOT Energy acceleration; prior BW8 catalogue tagged 32 Pokémon as ramp despite only six printed ramp effects: Victini-EX #18/#131, Manaphy #34, Vanilluxe #37, Magnezone #47, Blastoise #137. Twenty-six false ramp roles have been removed. Opposing control and status tags must not rely on arbitrary 'discard' / 'poisoned' substrings.
- Verification: 96/96 local independent BW6/BW7/BW8 tests pass across the selected seven suites, including 10 newly added BW8 checklist regressions; 315/315 independently generated BW8-only 30-card CPU decks were legal (9 type choices × 35 seeds). Version v0.69.313 is not yet device-tested or verified through a GitHub APK build; the user previously confirmed v0.69.312 built. Full manual review remains outstanding.
- The APK build gate and scripts/prepare-bw8.sh must run tests/v07313-plasma-storm-checklist.test.cjs alongside the two original BW8 suites. Commit pinned compact BW8 data plus generated JS/coverage to permit deterministic offline rebuilds.

2026-11-20 / v0.69.315 / PLASMA FREEZE BW9 INDEPENDENT MASTER-CHECKLIST AUDIT

The source generator and 119 enabled card effect schemas are checked.
- Ultra Ball (#122) and Superior Energy Retrieval (#103) require TWO OTHER physical hand cards as cost, chosen separately by UID. A card-reference is not an object wrapping an `r` property. Ultra Ball takes exactly the selected Pokemon card from deck. Superior Energy Retrieval recovers up to four eligible Basic Energy cards from PHYSICAL discard and/or the separate Basic Energy DISCARD LEDGER; cards discarded as its own cost cannot be recovered by that effect. Do not use the fresh Energy Zone as a discard source. Honor hand cap.

Chandelure Flare Navigate searches by Basic Energy subtype, not an optional `energyType` field absent on many older-set Basic prints; attaches from DECK, not the fresh ordered Zone.

BOTH sources must be moved to the physical draw deck on resolution, not just zeroed from the ledger. Preserve physical original card UID, make each returned ledger unit a real draw-deck Energy ref, then shuffle once.
- Muk Sludge Crash (#46) must log all coin outcomes and offer a fresh, explicit player choice of WHICH opposing attached Energy to discard for every heads. Repeat choices against the remaining legal sources; never silently discard the first source.
- Miltank Max Milk (#93) was independently tested; existing code correctly heals the chosen Pokemon but discards the attacker Miltank's own Energy. Preserve this behaviour. Kingdra Tri Bullet chooses up to three DISTINCT opponent Pokemon; each receives its printed damage exactly once.
- A valid catalogue schema and legal CPU deck alone are not a full printed-effect proof: generic BW9 smoke exercised 128/370 synthetic actions; 242 were skipped, while 88 manual-review flags remain. Review flags are not proof of defects. Device UI / prize timing and real match AI behaviour require separate checks.

Never claim Android/GitHub build success before user confirmation.

2026-11-20 / v0.69.317 / PLASMA BLAST COMPLETION PASS
- Implement new physical-choice rules for Masquerain Tool Reversal (selected own Tool to hand), Cradily Lifesplosion (selected Stage 2 from deck, up to Energy *cards* attached and Bench space), Accelgor Retribution (only if opponent attack KO'd an Escavalier on the previous turn), Uxie Psypower (three chosen damage counters), Azelf Psyjamming (individually move opponent-owned physical Special Energy to their other Pokemon), Porygon-Z Plasma Transfer (selected exact Plasma Energy UID), and Dusknoir Sinister Hand (one damage counter, picked source and destination, repeatable).
- The discarded Restored Lileep/Tirtouga/Archen must provide a legal once-per-turn Prehistoric Call from the DISCARD menu and move the exact reference to the BOTTOM of the draw deck. Exeggcute Propagation moves the exact printed discarded copy into its owner's hand, subject to the LuckyDex hand limit. The actions must not be listed as activatable board Abilities. The UI exposes these abilities in the discard overlay.
- CPU Fossil line building: a Restored Lileep requires Root Fossil Lileep #87; Tirtouga requires Cover Fossil #79; Archen requires Plume Fossil #82. Their legal Stage 1 evolutions Cradily, Carracosta, and Archeops do not bypass these Item entry paths. Do not use fossil Trainers as filler when corresponding Restored species are absent.
- Plasma Blast Red Signal only triggers on a physical Plasma Energy attached to Genesect-EX FROM HAND; it offers optional switching. Jirachi-EX Stellar Guidance is a hand-to-Bench trigger, not a deck-to-Bench trigger. Salamence Breakwing is a hand evolution trigger and discards all opponent-owned Tools. Do NOT put their rule names on the passive no-op list.
- Verdant Wind grants all allied Pokemon with Grass-providing Basic/Special Energy global Special Condition immunity and removes their existing conditions while unsuppressed, regardless of Pokemon type. Mental Shroud removes Weakness only with Uxie + Azelf in play. Badge of Discipline removes Fighting resistance. Cursed Glare prohibits opponent attachment of physical Special Energy from hand while Froslass is Active. Drifting Balloon modifies attack cost without altering printed text.
- Keep BW10 #33 Crush and Burn, #41 Toolbox, #86 Reversal Trigger, and #95 Scoop Up Cyclone gated until physical Energy-source selection, loss-of-Ability Tool selection, exact on-KO Prize ordering, and whole-stack/attachment return respectively are fully verified. Gated prints must not enter CPU or manual decks.
- Integration v0.69.317 regression tests execute after pinned BW10 catalogue generation in the Gradle APK gate; no extra Termux preparation command is needed. Device APK and full on-device card-by-card interaction testing still must be performed separately.

LuckyDex v0.69.319 — BW11 Legendary Treasures checklist extension (2026-11-20)
- Every set has a print-conservation check: BW11=115 numbered +25 Radiant Collection (RC1–RC25); 220 attacks and 19 Abilities from pinned source. Keep RC collector identities distinct from numbered prints and preserve exact image/card IDs.
- Battle-ready means all the printed nonblank attack, Ability, Trainer and Energy effects have working engine handling; otherwise mark the entire print gated, show reasons, and exclude it from auto-generated player/CPU decks. Numbered print count is not the number battle-enabled.
- Physical Special Energy represents one card with potentially more than one energy unit (Double Colorless = two Colorless units). Never allow the Basic Energy Zone to satisfy a discard of a physical named Special Energy such as Plasma Gale.
- Optional targeted Energy movement/discard and distributed damage must show provenance-aware source/target choice, retain UID references for physical Special Energy, and never reroll coins or draw a second deck card when resuming a choice.
- Continuous abilities are evaluated on each relevant board with suppression respected. Includes Royal Heal between turns; Garbotoxin suppression, Leaf Tailor Weakness immunity with Energy, Smooth Coat, Dark Cloak, Royal Garden, and Magic Room while Active. Abilities that require unimplemented global KO/coin triggers remain gated.
- BW11 single-set fallback must validate exactly 30 owned cards, maximum four copies, no illegal evolution lines, ACE SPEC cap, payable attacks and a matching typed Energy Zone. Keep it isolated to BW11 and do not override normal builder on successful builds.
- Official source generation and corresponding APK test gate must run in GitHub before asset merge; the user installs a source-relative ZIP using unzip -d source followed by git add/commit/push, with no manual preparation script. Locally skipped source-dependent tests are not counted as passed.

XY FLASHFIRE v0.69.327 — 19-PRINT COMPLETION / ADDITIONAL SHARED MECHANICS
- Flashfire 110 distinct collector rows remain immutable, including Blacksmith 88a and the Mega/full-art prints. - Leaf Draw discards an explicitly selected physical Basic Grass Energy from hand before drawing three; one use each owner turn. Whiplash repeatedly flips a coin and lets the attacker discard an explicitly selected opposing attached Basic/Special Energy for each heads until tails, preserving each physical card identity.
- Scorching Fang offers base attack or optional Fire-providing attached Energy discard; only a paid discard grants +30. Rock Rush offers the exact subset of hand-held Basic Fighting Energy to discard BEFORE dealing 30 damage per card. Do not choose hand cards for the player automatically, infer discarded cards from the Energy Zone, or silently consume any Special Energy.
