A ledger type count of 2 means TWO distinct Energy units, not one option. Finish all attachments before KO/Prize selection, then award opponent the proper Prize and promote as normal. Never allow a Pokémon-EX recipient.
- Rescue selects and shuffles three actual discarded Pokémon cards to the deck, retaining physical UIDs. Fang Snipe reveals the opponent's real hand and allows the attacking player to select one Trainer to discard. Revival benches a selected Basic Pokémon from the OPPONENT's discard, enforcing their Bench limit. Cursed Drop places four individually targeted damage counters on opposing Pokémon.
- Shadow Void repeatably moves one counter from another of your Pokémon onto Dusknoir (not from Dusknoir to itself); Pain Pellets chooses an opposing Pokémon and places damage counters equal to its current damage counters. Ear Influence lets the player distribute existing opposing damage counters between DIFFERENT opposing Pokémon, never create new counters.
- Flash Claw must request a real HAND-card selection from the DEFENDING player, not the attacker. Goodra Gooey Regeneration repeatedly discards selected attached Basic or physical Special Energy to heal 60; it is not once per turn. Furret Buried Treasure Hunt takes two explicitly selected physical cards from the top four of deck to hand and discards the other two. Lopunny Big Jump returns the entire evolution stack, Tools, physically attached Special Energy and Basic Energy to hand, respecting the app's hand cap and preserving UIDs as engine rules permit.
- Magnezone-EX dual 50-damage attack uses native two-distinct-Pokémon target choices; do not automatically redirect to the Active. Both ordinary and full-art Magnezone-EX use the identical effect. The opponent's Basic protections, HP and the two separate targets still apply. Basic-only separate targeting must not be conflated with bench-only splash damage.

CLASSIC BATTLE + BUILD v0.69.328 — USER-REPORTED REGRESSIONS
- All attack costs must normalise legacy array-form Energy symbols before displaying, labelling, or testing payment. [C,C] means CC, not C,C; [R,R,C] means RRC, not R,R,C. Commas are NEVER Energy symbols. Every attached Energy of any type can pay a Colourless unit, after satisfying required typed symbols. Do not silently rewrite canonical printed attack costs.
- Reshiram BW1 26/113 and BW4 21: Outrage CC 20+ (10 extra per own damage counter), Blue Flare RRC 120 (discard two attached Fire Energy). Two attached Fire Energy fully pay Outrage; three Fire or two Fire + any other attached Energy pay Blue Flare.
- Noble Victories Vanilluxe 29 Double Freeze uses shared coinDamageStatus: EXACTLY two logged visual flips, 40 damage per heads (0/40/80), and Paralysis if at least one heads. Use effects that log coin events so the battle UI can animate the result before prizes.

CLASSIC BATTLE v0.69.329 — GLOBAL PRINTED ATTACK ENERGY CONTRACT
- All Classic attack cost data MUST be compact typed Energy symbols (G R W L P F D M Y C), never comma-separated or an array at consumer boundaries. Normalize once at registration, reject unknown symbols; preserve official printed types and counts.
- One symbol consumes one provided Energy unit. Any typed Basic Energy pays a Colorless (C) requirement; pure Colorless Energy cannot pay printed typed requirements. Double Colorless contributes two C units. Compatible special/wild Energy may substitute named types only according to their attachment conditions; never double-spend a single physical unit.
- Battle action legality, attached Energy explanations, UI icons, AI energy valuation and deck builder all use the same canonical cost. Fairy Energy (Y) is pink Fairy, not Colorless; Fairy Basic Energy and its actual icon are supported.
- Audit each future deck across every attack cost, both typed and Colorless payment, exactly matching energy icons, runtime menu and available pinned-source print records. Run `node --test tests/v07329-classic-global-energy-audit.test.cjs` and keep the APK preBuild audit enabled.
- Full-Classic offline audit at v0.69.329: 9,725 moves across 59 set IDs; 508 previously array-shaped costs in Plasma Storm, Plasma Freeze and Plasma Blast normalized. 15 locally bundled source-print sets showed zero printed cost mismatches. XY must be generated before the final Gradle audit.

2026-11-21 / v0.69.333 / CLASSIC ALL-ERA DECK QUALITY AUDIT
=======================================================================
[LOCKED] CROSS-ERA AUTOBUILD CONTRACT (not only newly added sets)
  - Decks must contain exactly 30 real, owned, battle-ready cards and legally coherent evolution families, with sufficient Energy Zone types for intended attackers. A genuine Bench-only support Pokémon should not force dead third/fourth Energy types just because its unused attack is off-type.
  - Auto-selection of Trainer fillers should consider marginal role synergy. Prefer a spread of real usable draw, Pokémon search, evolution support, recycling, retreat/healing, and appropriate counterplay instead of defaulting to four copies of the top few generic Trainers. Four copies are NOT intrinsically illegal or banned, especially when a single-set print pool has only one or two useful Trainer cards; retain any user-selected legal four-copy package.
  - Printed target conditions must be respected: Giovanni/Brock/etc Trainers only with the corresponding named Pokémon, Rare Candy with relevant Stage 2 targets, Apricorn Maker only if a Ball card is included, Heavy Ball only if a compatible target exists, LV.X/SP support only with actual targets, and other role dependencies validated from printed text or existing executable effect metadata. Don't allow meaningless Stadium/Tool filler to crowd out the core line.
  - Recognise Trainers implemented via legacy neoRule/printed text, not only generic effect handlers; this information must feed both ranking and post-build synergy reporting.
  - ACE SPEC total limited to one; Miracle Energy limited to one per deck; no generic special Energy filler without a payoff. Basic Energy continues to come from the hybrid Energy Zone, not physical deck padding.
  - Prefer an initial 2-copy ceiling for AUTOMATIC generic Trainers, with an adaptive 3/4-copy fallback only if inventory cannot complete a coherent 30-card deck. Early sets with extremely small Trainer inventories may exceed the usual 18 Pokémon soft cap to complete a legal deck. Manually retained copies are exempt from soft caps.
  - Permanent test: `node --test tests/v07333-classic-deck-quality.test.cjs`, enabled in GitHub preBuild after the entire attack-energy audit. Current locally bundled Classic catalogue: 59 fully populated set IDs with two styles each = 118, plus six focused Trainer builds, ten mixed-era builds, eight CPU samples and two retention/support-specific builds, no invalid/missing Energy, dead-effect Trainer, ownership or 30-card failures. The three build-time generated BW11, XY1 and XY2 source catalogues were empty in local archive and are instead gated by their separate upstream generator-specific audits. Never represent this as proof of an installed APK or real CPU match quality.
  - Keep full player/autobuilder and CPU deck selection logic in step: audit opponent deck generation too when changing shared candidate/ranking logic. Don't use any unrequested Codex tools.

[LOCKED] TERMUX EXAMPLE FOR NEXT HANDOFF (source-relative ZIP)

    cd ~/lucky-dex

[LOCKED] RULES THAT APPLY TO FUTURE EXPANSIONS

  - Forcing opponent Supporters with attacker-owned choices is NOT equivalent to playing your own Supporter. Keep Hypno #36 Hand Control marked PARTIAL/deck-ineligible until this cross-player interaction exists. Patrat #84 Safety Check IS playable with a private reveal in the choosing player’s option pane and no public card-identity log. Build-time print coverage must report any additional unsupported text.

reduces its holder's retreat cost by two per attached copy; off-type evolution
must discard it. Double Colorless Energy contributes TWO Colorless units.

convertedEnergyCost or total count. Apply this comparison to all 124 XY4
prints after GitHub generates the official catalogue; keep the exact print,
spell/attack and deck gating tests enabled. This repairs the stage-1 CI-only
false-negative (five of six prior XY4 tests passed). The repair is audit-only:
no Pokémon abilities, attack rules, costs, or deck-building decisions change.

=== v0.69.342: XY Phantom Forces full-completion audit / 124 official prints ===
- The pinned official `xy4` catalogue has exactly 124 records, 162 printed attacks, and 15 Ability print occurrences, including alt prints 24a and 65a. Do not claim full set completion if GitHub's generated-catalogue audit fails or if any card remains gated.
- All attacks must use their precise printed Energy symbols (Classic may store cost as a compact string), and all printed mandatory effects must be actual, target-aware mechanics, not a silent noop.
- Venomoth Dizzying Wind: each Trainer played by the opponent on the next turn gets a separate coin check; tails prevents the Trainer effect but consumes/discards the card and appropriate once-per-turn usage.
- Pachirisu Trick Sticker: temporarily override only the Defending Pokemon's Weakness to Lightning through the end of the user's next turn, without changing the weakness multiplier. End when the target leaves the Active position.
- Heliolisk Parabolic Spark: the attacker chooses how many eligible Lightning Energy cards to discard before attack damage is decided (zero is allowed); each genuine physical/attached Energy discard increases damage by 30; the choices resolve before Prize handling.
- Head Ringer and Jamming Net are Team Flare Tools on the *opponent's* Pokemon-EX without an existing Tool. Head Ringer increases that Pokemon's attack costs by one Colorless; Jamming Net reduces its attack damage to defending Pokemon by 20 before Weakness/Resistance. Discarded opponent-attached Tools return to the actual card owner's discard. CPU follows the same legal choices.
- Robo Substitute is an Item in hand and deck, but once played becomes a 30 HP Colorless Basic-like Bench Pokemon. It cannot Retreat, can be discarded voluntarily by its owner before the attack, counts as a Knock Out when KO'd but never grants a Prize. It is not eligible as an opening Basic.
- Jellicent Ensnaring Spray adds 10 damage per Energy card genuinely attached to the opponent's Active Pokemon (including attached Special Energy); Galvantula Cobweb Trip chooses the opponent Bench Pokemon to switch into Active, then Confuses the newly Active Pokemon.
- All Phantom Forces alternate prints must preserve their real attack costs, attacks and Ability names; legitimate source-text differences in decorative rule wording are not gameplay differences.
- Permanent tests/v07342-phantom-forces-final-audit.test.cjs audits actual battle-state transitions locally and requires all 124 generated official prints and legal 30-card decks on GitHub. The Gradle auditXY4ForApk task MUST depend on generation and run this audit before Android assets merge.

GitHub Actions run #643 built v0.69.354 successfully. Stage 3 retains the fixed official XY6 112-print / 147-attack / 15-Ability / 12-Ancient-Trait catalogue and the immutable upstream Git blob. ## Newly battle-ready prints & mechanics

- Shaymin-EX #77, #77a and #106: Set Up is an optional triggered Ability only for a Shaymin placed from the hand onto your Bench. Show a draw-to-six or decline choice. Draw only from the physical deck, obey the Classic eight-card hand ceiling and deck exhaustion. Do not trigger for deck-to-Bench effects or when the hand already has six or more. Ability suppression still applies. Sky Return already used a verified return-to-hand implementation.
- Double Dragon Energy #97: One physical Special Energy card may be attached to **Dragon-type Pokémon only**. While valid, it provides two independent flexible Energy units (Miracle:2), not all individual types simultaneously, not two physical cards and never generated Basic Energy. Both must be spendable on different typed attack requirements; other Special-Energy interactions see one physical card. On becoming non-Dragon, discard the actual attached ref. Crystal Beach and Miracle Wind interactions remain in the shared Energy logic.
- Healing Scarf #84: Heal the attached Pokémon by up to 20 **only when an Energy card enters that Pokémon from its owner's hand**, including a hand attachment through a card effect. Energy Zone, discard or deck attachments do not count. Respect any existing no-healing effects.
- Mega Turbo #86: Select a real Mega Evolution Pokémon and one eligible discarded Basic Energy, including the hybrid Basic Energy discard pool. Attach the energy; it is not an attachment from hand. If no Mega or available Basic Energy exists, do not offer a playable Trainer action.
- Trainers' Mail #92 and #92a: Inspect only the top four physical deck cards and optionally select one Trainer other than Trainers' Mail. Selecting it removes that actual card, and declining or finding no eligible card shuffles normally. The other cards return to deck; the alternate print is independently preserved.

## Deck-building / CPU rules

The existing CPU takes only legal attack and attachment actions, so Double Dragon can only be attached to Dragon Pokémon. Auto-builder reserves at most two owned/eligible Double Dragon Special Energy in decks with actual Dragon lines and excludes it from generic filler. It must not automatically add Mega Turbo without a Mega Evolution in the constructed deck. Healing Scarf needs a way to attach physical Energy from hand (for a generic deck this means Special Energy). Existing Spirit Link rules still pair their exact Mega/Primal forms.

## Not yet completed

Sky Field #89 requires a genuine **eight-slot Bench on each side** with visible/actionable slots 0–7 and a choice to discard Benched Pokémon down to five, with attached cards handled correctly, when Sky Field leaves play. The current battle UI is limited to five slots, and the LuckyDex Classic30 default normally uses three; do not enable a fake three/five-slot substitute. Other unique attacks and Abilities remain gated until each is tested.

# LuckyDex v0.69.357 — Roaring Skies Stage 5, remaining card interactions

## Baseline

Stage 4 (Sky Field) is the patch baseline and successfully built in GitHub Actions run #645. The new Stage 5 patch itself is not APK-verified until pushed and built. The 112 official prints remain pinned to original Git blob `5eab0c0148a5cb52dd7005628aa61c793ec0d930` and are generated during the APK build, not fabricated in the ZIP.

## Newly supported 17 prints

- Ninjask #10: Afterimage Assault searches up to two physical Ninjask from the draw deck and Benches them using player-selected slots, respecting available Bench space.
- Shedinja #11: Cursed Rain places a damage counter on every opposing Pokémon, then switches with a friendly Benched Pokémon; Hopeless Scream was already supported.
- Deoxys #33: Close Encounter draws two; Overdrive Smash gives only its next use +60 on the next own turn, not the current attack.
- Gallade-EX #34/#99: Piercing Prizes deals 50 plus 20 for each of the attacker's *remaining* Prize cards (five-card Classic variant), not opponent prizes.
- Jirachi #42: Diminutive Desire selects one from the top seven physical deck cards and shuffles the rest; Doom Desire discards all actual attached Energy and schedules a knockout of the defending Pokémon at the *end of the opponent's next turn*, following that Pokémon by UID if it moves.
- Dragonite #51: Max Wind on evolving from hand offers optional healing of one chosen friendly Pokémon; it does not fire on evolution from deck, and standard Ability suppression applies.
- Shelgon #56: Exoskeleton continuously reduces attacking damage to Shelgon by ten after matchup, respecting suppression.
- Latios-EX #58/#101: Fast Raid uses the existing permitted first-turn attack path; Light Pulse protects against opposing *attack effects only*, never damage. A cross-era engine fix distinguishes this from all-damage attack guards.
- M Latios-EX #59/#102: Sonic Ace discards two attached Energy and deals 120 damage to a selected opposing Pokémon, with correct bench targeting.
- Hydreigon-EX #62/#103: Dragon Road continuously reduces allied Dragon retreat costs by two only while a Stadium is in play; no reduction for non-Dragon or when suppressed.
- Zekrom #64: Energy Stream attaches one genuinely discarded Basic Energy, never pulling from the fresh Energy Zone.
- Swablu #73: Bind Wound heals a chosen friendly Pokémon by twenty; it is not hardwired to the attacker.
- Pidove #78: Homing Pidove inspects the actual top physical card and offers a genuine optional shuffle.

## Coverage and safeguards

This is a Stage 5 implementation, not a claim that all Roaring Skies cards are battle ready. Gated cards with unimplemented mechanisms remain excluded from CPU decks and autobuilds. Alternate prints are preserved as unique collection IDs and receive exactly the same printed attack logic.

`tests/v07357-roaring-skies-stage5.test.cjs` uses injected print-faithful fixtures, checks all 17 enabled prints, their effect validation and attack/Ability outcomes, and runs under the `auditXY6Stage5ForApk` Gradle preflight after the Stage 4 audit and official catalogue generator. Targeted XY6+XY5+deck-quality suites have been run locally. The new test includes effect-only protection vs attack damage, Dragon Road team scope, and independent Bench, discard and Energy ledgers. The full APK build must still be verified on GitHub.

# LuckyDex v0.69.358 — XY Roaring Skies Stage 6 (verified interactions)

## Baseline and coverage

GitHub Actions #646 completed successfully for v0.69.357 Stage 5. This ZIP builds on that source, retains the official pinned 112-print Roaring Skies catalogue (Git blob `5eab0c0148a5cb52dd7005628aa61c793ec0d930`) and enables **10 additional original/alternate prints**: #14, #23, #29, #48, #52, #53, #63, #80, and Wally #94/#107 (**10 prints total**).

### Card-by-card

- **Fletchinder #14 — Peck Off** removes every attached Pokémon Tool on the defending Active *before* damage calculation. A pre-damage effect tag avoids the old error where Hard Charm or other Tool protection reduced the hit before being removed.
- **Zapdos #23 — Raging Thunder** deals 120 damage to the defending Active, then precisely 40 damage to a player-chosen friendly Benched Pokémon. It cannot silently damage the Active instead.
- **Xatu #29 — Future Sight** allows choosing either player's physical deck and ordering its actual top five cards individually; **Stressful Eye** reveals the opposing hand and lets the attacker select a Trainer card to discard, rather than choosing randomly or asking the wrong player.
- **Klefki #48 — Look for Keys** reveals physical draw-deck cards in order until the first Item, takes that Item, and shuffles all other revealed cards back. Without an Item it safely reshuffles the deck without manufacturing one or freezing.
- **Dragonite #52 — Wrapped in Wind** selects zero, one or two actual *Basic Energy cards in hand*; multiple colors can be attached, optional choices may be declined, and no fresh Zone Energy is generated.
- **Altaria #53 — Song of Echoes** shuffles its owner's hand into their draw deck and draws based on both players' occupied Bench slots (including Sky Field's expanded eight-slot Benches, subject to the Classic hand cap).
- **Reshiram #63 — Turboblaze** is an Active-only, once-per-turn Ability that attaches a Fire Energy *from hand* to a chosen Dragon Pokémon. It is not offered when no Fire Energy is in hand, does not use unspent Zone Energy, and signals correct hand-attachment triggers.
- **Unfezant #80 — Strong Winds** shuffles all players' attached Tool/Special Energy cards and Basic Energy cards back into their respective physical decks without relocating or discarding the Pokémon; no false Energy Zone refill.
- **Wally #94/#107** selects a real non-EX Pokémon on the board and a compatible evolution from the physical deck; this bypasses only the usual first-turn/newly-played timing restrictions, not evolution prohibitions. Both prints are playable. The autobuilder avoids Wally unless the selected 30-card deck has a compatible parent/evolution pair.

The gating system remains authoritative: any printed card still lacking an effect is **not** enabled in battle or auto-generated CPU decks. Stage 6 is not the final all-112 print audit. In particular, Banette Tool Concealment, Absol Cursed Eyes, Togekiss Serene Grace, Electrode Energy Bomb, and Wide Lens require further integration.

## Tests and build

`tests/v07358-roaring-skies-stage6.test.cjs` exercises physical draw/discard choices, turn ordering, before-damage Tool removal, dual-Bench draw, multiple hand-Energy colors, Turboblaze activation, Wally first-turn evolution/EX restrictions, and both-side attachment resets. The `auditXY6Stage6ForApk` Gradle task runs after all five older XY6 stages and pinned catalogue generation and is tied to the APK prebuild.

Prior to bundling the patch, the Roaring Skies Stages 1–6 plus deck-quality suite passed 68, failed 0 and skipped 3 catalogue-dependent tests; Stage 6's expanded direct suite passed 16/16. The APK build has not yet run for v0.69.358.

## Termux installation

```sh
cd ~/lucky-dex
unzip -o /sdcard/Download/LuckyDex_patch_v0.69.358_ROARING_SKIES_STAGE6.zip -d source
git add source
git commit -m "LuckyDex v0.69.358 Roaring Skies Stage 6 interactions"
git push origin main
```

  Evolution Pokémon of either player, including Benched Pokémon.
  Counters are not damage from an attack; replacement ends the effect.
- Forest of Giant Plants #74: Grass Pokémon (determined from effective
  type) may evolve on the first turn and the turn they entered play.
  It does not globally remove evolution requirements or non-Grass timing.
- Hex Maniac #75/#75a: one Supporter blocks ALL Pokémon Abilities
  through the opponent's following turn, including subsequently played
  Pokémon and abilities used from the discard pile. The block ends on
  the original player's next turn. Ancient Traits are not Abilities.
- Dangerous Energy #82: only Darkness hosts; physical Special Energy
  provides one Darkness unit and places two counters on opposing
  attacking EX when its Darkness Active host takes attack damage, even
  if knocked out. Counter placement is not attacking damage.
- Flash Energy #83: only Lightning hosts; physical Special Energy
  provides one Lightning unit and removes Weakness during damage
  calculations, including Bench Weakness where Wide Lens applies.
- Moving #82/#83 to an invalid host causes a real discard; preserving
  ownership/identity is mandatory. No Energy Zone substitution is made
  for these two physical Special Energy cards.
- Autobuilder: avoid #82/#83 in incompatible typing strategies; avoid
  Faded Town in Mega-dependent plans and require Grass evolution
  synergy for Forest. Keep players' manually constructed decks intact.

LUCKYDEX v0.69.374 — MECHANIC-FIRST SET DELIVERY CONTRACT [LOCKED]
===============================================================================
DATE: 22 November 2026. USER-APPROVED WORKFLOW CHANGE. This section is the
canonical workflow for future expansions and supersedes the prior habitual
practice of implementing an entire set in nine or ten short stages.

[LOCKED] MECHANIC-FIRST SET DELIVERY CONTRACT
1. BEFORE CODING: inspect the latest supplied source and master TXT; obtain
   the complete next official set print list and card text from a version-pinned
   authoritative source. Keep unique printed IDs including alternates, EX,
   secret rares, Trainer, Special Energy and unusual evolution forms.
2. Produce a WHOLE-SET EFFECT INVENTORY, not a batch-by-batch card list:
   (A) direct existing descriptor/engine handler; (B) a composition of
   existing reusable descriptors; (C) same mechanic with different typed
   parameters; (D) genuinely new shared engine mechanic; (E) card-specific
   exception requiring dedicated logic. Count each category before changes.
3. FIRST IMPLEMENT A/B/C for the entire expansion, reusing existing handlers
   with the correct target, timing, randomness, provenance, damage formula,
   coin outcomes, optional choice, resource zone and ownership. Compose shared
   effect primitives rather than duplicating every card's code.
4. Implement genuine D mechanics ONCE as shared engine support; check whether
   later cards/sets can reuse it. Only add dedicated E handlers where printed
   rules cannot be represented without compromising correctness. Avoid an
   engine rewrite or changes to unrelated minigames.
5. NEVER substitute noop, approximated damage, fabricated random choices or
   a card with a similar name for unsupported printed text. Do NOT enable a
   card with unsupported printed effects. A mapped attack alone is NOT enough
   to enable a print with an unimplemented Ability, evolution, Stadium, Tool
   passive, Special Energy triggered effect or game-state interaction.
6. Give human and CPU identical legal options and AI follow-through: target
   selection, Supporter choice, coin flip display, Energy source/destination,
   discard-zone physical identity, damage/status, cleanup, Prize timing.
   Rules stay compatible with existing LuckyDex Classic 30-card / Energy-Zone
   adaptations. The Zone is NOT a substitute for printed real Special Energy.
7. Aim for ONE substantial set implementation patch; follow with ONE
   comprehensive audit/correction patch when needed. Don't artificially split
   10 nearly identical implementations across 10 user turns. Genuine new
   engine mechanics justify a separate, clearly scoped pass if necessary.
8. For each set, permanently add a print-coverage ledger and automated tests
   for every source print, attack, Ability, Trainer, Tool, Stadium, Special
   Energy and named mechanics. Assert no broken old behaviour on earlier
   sets, standard card/energy deck-building quality, genuine evolution lines,
   CPU legality, and target/choice timing. Build on CI, fix compile failures
   before declaring APK success; local tests ≠ GitHub success ≠ phone test.
9. The next assistant must report EXACT numbers from the generated catalogue:
   printed total, battleEnabled, gated and reasons; never claim 'complete'
   unless there are zero gated prints AND tests passed. Distinguish collection
   visibility, playable battle support, local audit and actual APK CI.
10. Delivery contract: ship a source-relative changed-files-only ZIP, explain
    extraction path, provide a working Termux snippet (cd ~/lucky-dex;
    unzip -o /sdcard/Download/<PATCH>.zip -d source; git add source;
    git commit -m "..."; git push origin main). No Codex unless requested.
11. For mechanic-first handoff between chats: the current set's verifier lives
    at source/scripts/<set>_verified.py, catalogue generator at
    source/scripts/build-<set>.py, print ledger at source/docs/<SET>_BUILD_COVERAGE.json
    after build, and tests under source/tests/. Read these before work.
12. Do not treat old release notes marked [CURRENT] as more authoritative
    than this new [LOCKED] workflow. Preserve all historical audits as evidence.

2026-11-22 — XY8 BREAKthrough COMPLETE v0.69.376: 165/165 PRINTS
================================================================================
- The mechanic-first, reuse-first contract remains permanent. This release
  completes all 165 distinct XY8 print IDs (including alternate artwork),
  220 printed attacks, 19 Abilities and all six BREAK Pokémon. BREAK shares
  the previous-card inheritance mechanisms already used for LV.X, but uses
  ordinary evolution timing and may evolve on the Bench.
- The build-time generator MUST fail when ANY XY8 print has missing printed
  attack, Ability, Trainer, Ancient Trait or Special Energy coverage. The
  generated-catalogue Node test MUST require exactly 165 battleEnabled and
  deckEligible authentic prints. Never skip this generated-catalogue test
  during GitHub Actions; source-only local tests can skip it.
- Continue to reuse existing exact mechanics, then extend them at shared
  resolution/choice/status hooks. Newly finished XY8 groups include physical
  target/zone choices, full-attack Energy discard choices, once-per-turn and
  recurring Abilities, source-specific flip logs, opponent-first recovery,
  Energy redistribution, active-only Dragon Dance boost, and conditional
  healing based on actual damage removed this turn.
- Burning Energy (#151) is an actual special Energy card attachable only to
  Fire Pokémon. After an attack from that Pokémon DISCARDs that exact attached
  physical Burning Energy card, return the SAME UID after attack resolution,
  including any prompted choices, only if the Pokémon remains in play and
  still has Fire typing. Other discard events NEVER cause this return.
- Rainbow Energy (#152) contributes ONE flexible Energy unit rather than
  one of every type simultaneously; attach from hand puts 1 damage counter
  on the attached Pokémon. Do not consume the Energy Zone attachment for
  an additional Ability-granted hand Energy attachment.
- Parallel City (#145) offers two distinct orientations when played. The
  printed 3-Pokémon Bench restriction affects one side, and the other side
  has -20 damage from Grass/Fire/Water attacks. Preserve stadium ownership
  and selected orientation; three-Bench is already the app's default limit.
- Heavy Boots (#141): only Pokémon with printed Retreat Cost 3+ gain +20 HP
  and cannot be Confused; remove existing Confusion when attached. Do not
  create a generic all-Pokémon HP boost.
- Never mark a card fully implemented merely because its descriptor passes
  validateEffect(). Run explicit battle-state outcome checks (UI choices,
  physical card UIDs, Energy/KO/turn timing, opposing player choices, Tools,
  deck builder legality). Use one local broad regression run and one final
  GitHub CI build before declaring the APK confirmed.
- CI regressions from v0.69.375 were fixed: Xerneas #107's Power Creation
  needs the existing healed-this-turn predicate; version-consistency audit
  must see the identical build in Gradle, HTML title and boot JavaScript.
- LOCAL VERIFICATION: 257 passed / 0 failed / 10 skipped in the broad XY-era
  regression selection; generated catalogue verification runs in GitHub only.
  An independent official XY8 catalogue comparison found 165/165 mapped and
  no remaining unmapped attacks/Abilities/Trainers/Energy cards. This is
  NOT a claim that the GitHub APK build has already succeeded.

2026-11-22 — BREAKthrough independent audit v0.69.377 / MASTER v2.60
================================================================================
Audit basis: successful v0.69.376 GitHub run 45020904916 and source for
all 165 XY8 official prints. The successful v0.69.376 build verifies the
completion gate; it does NOT by itself verify every effect outcome.

AUDIT FINDING 1 — BREAK and LV.X INHERITED PASSIVE ABILITIES
- The shared upgradeAbilities(slot,cardById) resolver already retains
  previous evolution Abilities, but modifierAmount and passiveProtection
  previously read only the current card's abilities.
- This silently dropped old continuous passives on the upgraded card.
  Concrete XY8 example: Florges BREAK (xy8-104) MUST retain Florges
  Calming Aroma, reducing friendly Fairy attack Energy costs, as well
  as gaining Floral Breeze. Do not implement a Florges-only workaround.
- Reuse upgradeAbilities for both own-team and opposing passive modifier
  loops AND for passive protection checks. Respect Ability suppression and
  prevent accidental double application; regression tests required.

AUDIT FINDING 2 — PARALLEL CITY AFTER SKY FIELD
- When Parallel City (xy8-145) replaces Sky Field (xy6-89), the selected
  player must discard their own extra Benched Pokemon until THREE remain,
  preserving the card owners' right to choose and discarding all attached
  cards without a Knock Out or Prize award. The other player retains their
  normal post-Sky Field cap (five in LuckyDex Classic).
- Reuse Sky Field's existing physical bench-trim choice UI instead of
  auto-selecting which Pokemon to discard. Parallel City normally has no
  bench-size effect if the app is already enforcing its default three.
- IMPORTANT CROSS-SET ORDERING: Sky Field leaving for NARROW GYM must
  still first discard to FIVE, then Narrow Gym returns down to its limit.
  Do not make the shared Sky Field trim use Narrow Gym's cap directly;
  the older Roaring Skies regression checks this explicitly.

AUDIT FINDING 3 — VERSION-TEST DRIFT
- Old tests must not embed an old literal version like 0.69.376: they
  must read Gradle versionName dynamically and match title UI and boot JS.
- Add an independent v07377 audit to the generated XY8 Gradle gate;
  preserve the strict 165/165 catalogue audit in GitHub CI.

VERIFICATION PERFORMED:
- v07333–v07359 selected tests: 278 total, 259 passed, 0 failed, 19 skipped
- v07360–v07369 selected tests: 82 total, 78 passed, 0 failed, 4 skipped
- v07370–v07377 selected tests: 92 total, 90 passed, 0 failed, 2 skipped
- Overall: 452 tests, 427 passed / 0 failed / 25 skipped locally.
- Skips primarily involve pinned upstream catalogues generated by CI.
- The current audit patch APK is NOT claimed verified until its GitHub build.

NEXT SET: XY—BREAKpoint (xy9), after the successful v0.69.377 BREAKthrough audit.

90f27d0e14cefe48ccb2b8922a7a356b2fcf0072,
card blob SHA dcfa9ec1b6b0ff6a0b446114c9d567c598cf71ce.

STANDING MECHANIC-FIRST RULE (CARRIED FORWARD):
  * Search the prior LV.X, BREAK, Mega and all-era effect engine before claiming
    a new mechanic. The five BREAKpoint BREAK evolve through the SAME audited
    previous-card LV.X/BREAK infrastructure. Keep printed differences intact.
  * Implement a shared mechanic once; re-use the previous DSL effect when its
    timing, zone, owner, target, choice and actual cards match. If it does not,
    gate the whole card instead of claiming it playable with a fake no-op.
  * In Classic 30-card battle, Basic Energy is represented by the separate
    Energy Zone, NOT ordinarily by physical cards in the 30-card draw deck.
    Attacks saying 'search deck for Basic Energy' must use the verified Energy
    Zone fallback as well as physical Basic Energy when present. BREAKpoint
    Gyarados-EX Stormy Seas is a tested example; card selection moves actual
    physical refs when supplied by deck, but searches Energy Zone otherwise.
  * Preserve the user's requirement that player choices be explicit, attacks
    and all effects resolve BEFORE Prize card selection, and CPU receives the
    same legal operations. Do not silently attach fake Energy or choose for a
    human when multiple legal targets exist.

THIS INTEGRATION:
  * Pinned generator source/scripts/build-xy9.py verifies exact 126/159/14/5.
  * Only audited cards are battle-enabled. Coverage projected approx. 91/126;
    the GitHub-generated report docs/XY9_BUILD_COVERAGE.json is authoritative.
  * Shared effect mappings live in scripts/xy9_verified.py. Alternate-art
    reprints share *effects*, but retain their separate print IDs.
  * New choices: Stormy Seas Water attachments from physical deck OR Energy
    Zone, and M Gyarados-EX Blast Geyser optional bonus/discard. Both have
    independently tested battle outcomes. Manaphy Aqua Tube and Trevenant
    Nervous Seed use shared continuous stat modifiers.
  * Gyarados and Scizor Spirit Links reuse existing Mega evolution exemption.
    Never autobuild a Spirit Link without its matching Mega attacker.
  * Do NOT call remaining 35 printed cards complete. Major gated groups:
    Garbodor/Greninja global Ability suppression, Slowbro instant win, mixed
    orientation Reverse Valley, twin-Puzzle-of-Time resolution, Splash Energy
    KO returns, physical hand/discard choices and activated basic-energy limits.
    Ho-Oh-EX Purifying Fire remains gated until BASIC Fire attachment is proven;
    a Special Energy with Fire provision must not satisfy that requirement.

PREVENT REPEATED GITHUB BUILD FAILURES:
  * The early all-era deck-quality test runs BEFORE generator for newly added
    sets. Add every new XY set to emptyLocalCatalogues in
    tests/v07333-classic-deck-quality.test.cjs, with a test verifying the order.
  * Run the set-specific generated-catalogue audit AFTER generateXY9ForApk.
    On GitHub the test MUST require complete printed identity + supported
    attack effects. Local source-only stub skips precisely that test.
  * Runtime attack costs may be a compact STRING (e.g. 'WCC'), not an ARRAY;
    independent audits must accept either while checking the printed cost.
  * Keep Gradle, HTML title version and app boot version synchronized.
  * Test both player and opponent autogenerated 30-card decks for Energy,
    evolution lines and dead Trainers after generated cards are available.

       python3 scripts/preflight-xy9-release.py

     generateXY9ForApk; auditXY9ForApk runs generated tests AFTER it. New sets
     MUST adopt this two-stage, source-only + generated approach, not one test
     copied from the previous stage with outdated hardcoded gated lists.
  6. Require ALL prior-era and cross-set audit suites, both deck-builder styles,
     basic/special Energy ledger, opponent AI, staged evolution inheritance,
     timing, player choice, metadata and UI versions. Do NOT ship when any
     unexpected test fails or catalogue tests are skipped in generated mode.
  7. Before delivery, inspect APK build gate dependency ordering in Gradle;
     run the source preflight and, whenever possible, the full generated
     preflight. After user's push confirm GitHub Actions before calling APK
     successful. If network/preconditions prevent full preflight, disclose it.
  8. NEVER remove or dilute audits solely to make CI green. Fix stale EXPECTED
     STATES to match fully tested printed behaviour, but preserve independent
     catalogue correctness, real battle outcomes and 30-card legal deck checks.

XY9 BREAKPOINT v0.69.381 — FINAL FOUR / DOC v2.65
================================================================================
Completes the official pinned BREAKpoint XY9 set, 126 of 126 printed variants.
Reuses old card engine paths; do not open a new attack-copy, top-deck,
paired-Item, or KO-return subsystem for equivalent future effects.

  #67 Sudowoodo Watch and Learn: replay the attack actually used by the
      opposing side LAST TURN, including its printed damage/effects and
      associated choices. Store the original attacking card ID; the opponent
      may have switched or evolved since the last turn. If no valid prior
      attack, do nothing, and cap copy depth to avoid recursion.
  #102 Max Elixir: top SIX physical deck cards only, target a chosen Basic
      Bench Pokémon; transfer one genuine Basic Energy attachment and shuffle
      deck. For this app's Classic Energy Zone, if there are no physical Basic
      Energy cards anywhere in deck, treat the next six ordered Zone units as
      the limited substitute; never search arbitrarily deep Zone supply.
  #109 Puzzle of Time: one-copy branch picks the top three cards in order;
      two-copy branch consumes TWO distinct physical Puzzle cards from hand
      then retrieves two chosen actual discard cards to hand. Neither Puzzle
      can retrieve itself during its own resolution. All nested choices must
      finish before player regains normal battle controls.
  #113 Splash Energy: Water Pokémon only, provides one Water. On KO CAUSED
      BY DAMAGE from an opposing attack, put the knocked-out Pokémon card
      into the owner's hand, discard the prior stages and all attachments,
      and award normal Prizes. No rescue for poison, damage-counter effects,
      own damage or an off-type recipient. Reuses HGSS Rescue Energy KO hook.

PERMANENT RELEASE-CHECK CONTRACT (past failures must not recur):
  * Update single source scripts/xy9_release_contract.json first: expected
    count 126, gated [], completion IDs 67,102,109,113.
  * Never carry old test assertions requiring these four prints to stay gated.
  * Always run: python3 scripts/preflight-xy9-release.py and node --test
    tests/v07381-breakpoint-completion.test.cjs plus previous stage suites.
  * Permanent full-engine KO regression: Basic Water and evolved Water with
    Splash Energy both return the top Pokémon card, discard attachments and
    earlier stages, still award the opponent a Prize, and require promotion.
  * Mandatory GitHub tasks: generateXY9ForApk -> auditXY9ForApk (including
    final outcome tests) -> preBuild. Build-time canonical print audit must
    verify 126/126 fully enabled, attack costs, 159 attacks, 14 Abilities,
    legal 30-card decks, version parity and zero contradictory gate IDs.
  * When pinned official source fetch cannot run locally, report that as a
    limitation, NOT as a passed full audit. The APK cannot be declared green
    until GitHub has actually built the new SHA successfully.

XY9 BREAKPOINT v0.69.382 — INDEPENDENT RULE AUDIT / DOC v2.66
================================================================================
Source audited: completed XY9 v0.69.381, 126/126 mapped prints. A successful
source-only audit does not prove the APK built; check GitHub CI for the pushed
SHA. Generated official 126-card coverage and two deckbuilder styles remain
mandatory post-generation checks. No older set is deliberately removed.

REPRODUCED GAMEPLAY BUG AND REPAIR:
  #88 Raticate — Antibodies prevents new Special Conditions, but its printed
  text ALSO requires that any existing Special Conditions be removed. When
  Garbodor's Garbotoxin suppresses Antibodies, a condition may be applied.
  Once the Tool is removed from Garbodor and the suppression ends, Antibodies
  must immediately clear existing Poison, Burn, Sleep, Confusion, and Paralysis.
  XY9 #89 Raticate BREAK must inherit this continuous cleanup as well as its
  passive immunity. Source of truth: XY9 verified Ability effect includes the
  explicit clearExisting flag, rather than using a generic preventStatus rule
  for every Pokémon that prevents new conditions.
  Engine: applyContinuousRules processes self-scoped preventStatus that
  explicitly opts into clearExisting, but only while its source Ability is
  not suppressed. Never automatically remove pre-existing conditions for an
  immunity whose printed text does not instruct such removal.

NEW PERMANENT AUDIT TESTS:
  tests/v07382-breakpoint-independent-audit.test.cjs asserts Overgrow's
  remaining-HP threshold, Golduck BREAK's finite Basic-Energy redistribution
  and Special-Energy exclusion, Raticate BREAK's inherited Antibodies,
  Garbotoxin suppression/restoration, Ferrothorn Spike Lash Active-vs-Bench
  accounting, and the actual Antibodies mapping.
  The test is mandatory in Gradle auditXY9ForApk and the full preflight.

CONTINUE TEST DISCIPLINE:
  * Test cross-card state changes, especially when a Tool removal reactivates
    an Ability. Checking only newly applied conditions is insufficient.
  * Verify Gradle versionCode = 7000 + patch component of versionName
    (v0.69.382 requires 7382). This caught a stale versionCode 7381
    during independent cross-set regression before sending the ZIP.
  * Run the standard source preflight, all XY9 completion and audit suites,
    selected XY8 and XY7 regressions, and APK generated-catalogue audit.
  * Do NOT claim full official 126-card audit locally if pinned catalogue
    generation has not run; zero skipped source-dependent tests in GitHub.
  * Preserve canonical master TXT in the same patch, source-relative ZIP.

APPENDIX — v0.69.384 FATES COLLIDE STAGE 2 + MANDATORY GENERATOR-ORDER RULE
================================================================================
KNOWN REAL GITHUB BUILD FAILURE: v0.69.383, GitHub run #45040073029.
  Cause: `preflightXY10ContractForApk` executes BEFORE generateXY10, but its
  nested `node --test` inherited GITHUB_ACTIONS=true. The strict generated-
  catalogue test then tried opening `data/classic/xy10-print-compact.json`
  before that file existed. This is not an invalid card mechanic.

HARD RULE FOR ALL FUTURE SETS AND ALL NEW CHAT CONTINUATIONS:
  1. Two distinct CI phases must be modeled explicitly. Source-only preflight
     must be runnable with GITHUB_ACTIONS=true on the OUTER process without
     enforcing the not-yet-generated catalogue within nested test runners.
     Remove GITHUB_ACTIONS from the child test environment for source-only
     checks, or use a dedicated explicit phase flag (do not fake data files).
  2. Run the immutable-source generator AFTER source-only preflight. Only then
     run the STRICT post-generation audit with GITHUB_ACTIONS=true. Never
     disable or skip it once the generator has run.
  3. Before every patch, run:
       GITHUB_ACTIONS=true python3 scripts/preflight-xy10-release.py
       node --test tests/v07383-fates-collide-integration.test.cjs \
         tests/v07383-fates-collide-effects.test.cjs \
         tests/v07384-fates-collide-stage2.test.cjs
     Adapt names for subsequent expansions. Never trust a test summary whose
     critical generated-catalogue test was skipped locally as proof of APK.
  4. As cards are enabled, keep ONE required-enabled release contract. Old
     gating assertions must be updated and replaced with behavioural tests,
     not merely deleted. Roll all previous required-enabled prints forward.
  5. When a card effect includes an OPTIONAL damage bonus, test its actual
     choice-resolution order. Validators alone will accept chooseOption +
     bonusDamage, but applying the bonus after attack damage is calculated can
     throw. Gate it until an actual battle-path test proves correct timing.
  6. Always audit continuous abilities on Active AND Bench, before/after
     Weakness/Resistance, inherited BREAK effects, and Ability suppression;
     Lugia's Pressure and Regirock-EX's Regi Power are reference cases.
  7. Keep all four version markers and previous/current set release contracts
     aligned. Changed-files-only ZIP relative to source; do not include any
     generated or untracked large art files.

Stage 2 new mechanisms and exact touched print numbers are documented in
  docs/XY10_FATES_COLLIDE_STAGE2_v0.69.384.md
  tests/v07384-fates-collide-stage2.test.cjs
  scripts/xy10_release_contract.json

No claims of full 129-print readiness. The full pinned catalogue must be
created and verified during GitHub CI before report of definitive coverage.

APPENDIX — v2.70 / v0.69.384 XY10 GENERATED-CATALOGUE CI REPAIR
=============================================================
Dated 2026-11-22. Critical lessons from repeated Fates Collide build failures:

A. FAILURE CAUSE (GITHUB RUN 45042878867)
  The Gradle syntax and source-only preflight passed, and the official 129-print
  Fates Collide catalogue was generated. Then auditXY10ForApk failed in
  v07383-fates-collide-integration.test.cjs at card.attacks.map(...). Pokemon
  have attacks arrays; Trainer and Energy runtime records generally do not.
  The official source may include a printed attack on a Pokemon Tool (Power
  Memory), but this is NOT a Pokemon attacks array. Fixed by applying attack
  cost comparisons and effect checks only to Pokemon; keeping trainer/energy
  validation separate; retaining all 129-print source and deck checks.

B. NON-NEGOTIABLE FULL PREFLIGHT BEFORE *EVERY* GIT PUSH
  The source-only preflight can give false confidence because the generated
  catalogue test is intentionally skipped until source generation. Hence
  passing only source-only unit tests NEVER establishes build readiness.
  From ~/lucky-dex, after unzipping a patch:

     cd ~/lucky-dex/source
     python3 scripts/check-gradle-source.py --self-test
     python3 scripts/preflight-xy10-release.py --full

  The --full command first runs source-only checks, then generates the pinned
  official xy10 data and runtime, then runs the exact three Node test files
  required by :app:auditXY10ForApk with GITHUB_ACTIONS=true. Any nonzero
  exit code, missing Node/Python installation, network fetch failure, failed
  deck assertion, failed card assertion, or skipped full catalogue verification
  BLOCKS commit and push. Never replace --full with a GITHUB_ACTIONS=false
  source-only run as evidence. The pinned source must remain source-of-truth.
  If offline/local tools cannot perform this full check, transparently say
  the APK build is UNVERIFIED and make the Termux full pre-push gate mandatory.
  Do not claim a successful full audit merely because source-only checks pass.

C. GENERATED MODEL TYPE SAFETY
  Check Pokemon/Trainer/Energy separately in all current and future generated
  catalogue audits: required fields and supported effects differ. Never call
  .attacks.map, .attacks.forEach or .abilities.map on all print records unless
  presence and type were verified. Runtime Pokemon .attacks should always be
  an array; no blank-array coercion may mask broken Pokemon data. A Trainer
  with a printed Tool-granted attack must not be treated as a Pokemon record.
  Add three-kind fixture tests to preflight for every new expansion.

D. RELEASE STATUS
  v0.69.383 failed due to premature generated-file access during preflight.
  v0.69.384 stage2 failed due to malformed Groovy audit commandLine.
  v0.69.384 syntax repair passed source-only checks but failed generated
  catalogue audit at Trainer/Energy .attacks.map. This patch corrects that
  failure; full GitHub APK success remains unverified until pushed.

E. POST-GENERATION REALITY CHECK
  Full preflight verifies original 129 prints, all 147 printed attacks, 21
  Abilities, five BREAK cards, correct print order and alternate-art records,
  per-print runtime mapping, absence of fake noop effects on enabled cards,
  required minimum/required-enabled set, and legal CPU/player deck building.
  New releases must run this before shipping; apply the same two-phase model
  to XY11 and all subsequent sets. Do not introduce a hardcoded stale-stage
  expectation that contradicts the current playable coverage contract.

FATES COLLIDE XY10 STAGE 4 — v0.69.386 / Master Rules v2.72 (2026-11-22)
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. The successful v0.69.385 APK run #45048155104 is the baseline. Stage 4
   enables seven additional distinct prints: 4 Mothim, 19 Omastar BREAK,
   23 Barbaracle, 61 Bronzong, 71 Whimsicott, 73 and 122 Kingdra-EX.
   The strict generated-catalogue floor advances from 92 to 99 of 129.
2. Every activated ability must use the same requestChoice/resolvingChoice
   round trip for human and CPU, with explicitly validated final targets.
   Omastar BREAK Dangerous Tentacle may select ONLY opponent Benched EX;
   Mega Catcher uses the same valid benchIndex/choiceBenchIndex contract.
3. Source/target restrictions matter: Wormadam First moves an EXISTING 10
   damage counter from a damaged Wormadam; Light Steps moves a REAL attached
   Basic Energy unit or preserves the UID of a physically attached Special.
   Kingdra's Dragon Trail counts Basic Lightning attached to THAT attacker,
   not Special Lightning units or unused cards in the Energy Zone.
4. Barbaracle Hand Block applies only while a Stadium is in play, only to
   the opponent's from-hand Special Energy attachments, and shuts off under
   Ability suppression. Bronzong Metal Fortress protects friendly BENCH
   only from OPPONENT attack effects, including damage. Do not block own
   recoil or opponent attack damage to the Active Pokémon.
5. `source/tests/v07386-fates-collide-stage4.test.cjs` is a mandatory local
   and post-generation GitHub APK test. The generated catalogue must enable
   the seven Stage 4 prints and keep unsupported prints gated. The release
   preflight must include all current stages, check the Gradle source syntax,
   and run with `--full` ON THE USER'S TERMUX before commit/push.
6. When exporting a ZIP, include ONLY changed paths under `source/` as a

## XY11 printed Energy-cost regression — v0.69.391b (22 November 2026)

Classic registration in `app/src/main/assets/tcg-classic-battle-data.js` intentionally converts each attack cost from the generator's `['G','C']` array to the runtime string `'GC'`. This is not an attack-cost bug. Any test importing the Classic battle registry or core before examining a generated set must compare exact printed costs using `Array.isArray(cost) ? cost.join('') : String(cost ?? '')` against the pinned source's `sourceCost.join('')` instead of comparing a string with an array. Check both single-unit ('C') and multi-unit ('GC') costs, including full 116-print generated tests. This should be a shared regression rule for all future sets, not another one-off patch.

A source-only preflight with skipped generated-catalogue checks is NOT proof of a clean `--full` preflight or GitHub build. Keep `preflight-xy11-release.py --full` mandatory before Termux commits/pushes. Do not modify actual attack energy prices or runtime card registration to make a malformed test pass.

=== v0.69.398 EVOLUTIONS (XY12) INDEPENDENT-AUDIT LESSONS ===
1. In the Classic battle engine, `resolveAttack` settles printed and optional base/bonus damage BEFORE pending `applyEffect` choices. Do NOT nest `bonusDamage`, `coinDamage`, `energyDamage` or `damageFromDamage` beneath an `applyEffect`-time `chooseOption`: that can throw or silently lose bonus damage. Attack damage alternatives must be exposed via `choicesForEffect` and used by `resolveAttack` itself; add regression tests for both outcomes and any Energy discard after dealing damage. Raichu XY12 #36 Spark Bolt is the reference contract.
2. “Attach an Energy card from your discard pile” MUST consider actual physical Special Energy cards, not only virtual Basic Energy counters. Preserve the Special Energy card reference (uid, cardId and proper provides) when attached. Maintain the distinction between a physical Basic Energy card in the discard and a discarded hybrid Energy Zone unit, and never attach Pokémon or Trainers. Mewtwo-EX XY12 #52/#103 Energy Absorption is the reference contract.
3. New-set independent audits must test both effects and printed print variants, plus multiple random player/CPU deck seeds. Add new independent-audit tests to both the set-specific local `--full` preflight AND the Gradle APK audit task. Do not sign off an APK based on local tests alone; confirm GitHub Actions after the user pushes.
4. For XY12, `scripts/preflight-xy12-release.py --full` and the GitHub `auditXY12ForApk` test task must include tests/v07398-evolutions-independent-audit.test.cjs after v07396 and v07397. Keep version contracts in XY9–XY12, UI, preflights and app/build.gradle consistent for every next release.
=== END v0.69.398 LESSONS ===

=== FIELD ATTACK FIXES — 23 NOVEMBER 2026 (v2.79; base APK 0.69.401) ===
1. [LOCKED] Primal Clash Groudon-EX Rip Claw (#85/#150) is a 30-damage attack even when the defender has zero Energy. The heads-only optional Energy discard must NEVER prevent attacking. In generic `coinFlip` attack-choice enumeration, an empty contingent effect target set must not discard the base attack action. A heads result with a real Energy does discard one; tails never discards.
2. [SUPERSEDED HISTORICAL RULE] The printed TCG Thunder Rod (#27/#87) does NOT flip coins. The original LuckyDex hybrid implementation exposed four Zone units without coin flips, but this was overpowered in monotype Zone decks. The user's newer v2.81 rule BELOW overrides this: four coin flips gate the attachments, capped by real Lightning units in the top four Zone units. Do not revert the user's requested balance adaptation.
3. [LOCKED] Ancient Origins M Ampharos-EX Exavolt (#28/#88) offers pre-attack BASE (120) or BOOST (170) choices. When BOOST is selected, Paralyse the opponent's Active and apply 30 recoil after the hit. Do NOT invoke `bonusDamage` from a post-damage `chooseOption`: `applyEffect` throws for attack damage effects. Implement alternative attack damage in `resolveAttack` and expose `effectSelection` options from `choicesForEffect`.
4. [LOCKED] `tests/v07402-field-reported-attack-fixes.test.cjs` is a regression required by APK `auditXY7ForApk` and proves zero Energy, heads, tails, both EX print variants and both damage branches. The overlay does NOT change app build number or release contracts; preserve coordinated versioning for the next set. Run relevant source preflights before pushing. Do not claim GitHub build success until confirmed externally.
5. [CURRENT] `docs/FIELD_REPORTED_ATTACK_FIXES_v0.69.401.md` records exact code paths and the intended patch overlay. Source-relative extraction remains `unzip -o PATCH.zip -d source`; no setup command is needed for these ordinary JS/Python/Gradle changes.
=== END FIELD ATTACK FIXES ===

=== WILD EDGE CPU DOUBLE-ATTACK HOTFIX — 23 NOVEMBER 2026 (v2.80) ===
[BUG] Steam Siege Steelix-EX (xy11-67) Wild Edge previously used post-damage chooseOption(BONUS(50), recoil(20)). The initial 80 hit happened, then accepting extra damage raised "bonusDamage must resolve as part of an attack". This stranded CPU effectChoice recovery and created apparent/repeated attacks. All optional +damage choices MUST be decided before the damage phase.
[LOCKED] Steelix-EX offers base 80 or boost 130 +20 recoil via xy11Rule:wildEdge action `effectSelection` preattack. Never reuse the Plasma Storm Braviary Wild Edge (bw8-116) values: Braviary is base 60 or boost 80 +20 recoil. Both printings must be tested independently and yield control after one attack.
[LOCKED] CPU error recovery must not retry a failed optional effect choice. Prefer decline/skip or a DIFFERENT legal action after restoring snapshot, and never repeat an erroring effectChoice indefinitely.
[LOCKED] `tests/v07403-wild-edge-cpu-turn.test.cjs` MUST run in Gradle auditXY11ForApk AND --full XY11 preflight, alongside mandatory v07402 field-reported regression. Test damage, recoil, turn handoff, no pending choice and exact one attack per turn for both Wild Edge cards.
[CURRENT] Use a single overlay for Groudon-EX Rip Claw, Ampharos-EX Thunder Rod, M Ampharos-EX Exavolt and Steelix-EX Wild Edge, not successive conflicting patches; keep versionName/versionCode 0.69.401/7401 until a coordinated release bump. Overlay is relative to `source/` and requires no prepare script.
=== END WILD EDGE CPU HOTFIX ===


=== THUNDER ROD HYBRID BALANCE OVERRIDE — 23 NOVEMBER 2026 (v2.81) ===
[USER-DIRECTED OVERRIDE] Ampharos-EX Ancient Origins (#27/#87) Thunder Rod is INTENTIONALLY changed from official printed TCG rules, because the hybrid Energy Zone otherwise makes four attachments automatic. Use FOUR fair coin flips ONCE at attack resolution; each heads may attach ONE Lightning unit from the four real top-of-Zone units revealed. Thus success is limited by BOTH heads count AND actual revealed Lightning supply. Never fabricate an Energy unit, never draw a physical deck card for this special hybrid acceleration, and return/shuffle all unused Zone units. Four tails = zero attachments; four heads with four available Lightning = four (rare). Partial heads and supply shortage must be tested. Do NOT revert to previous locked assertion at v2.79 that Thunder Rod must not flip; the user's balance override supersedes that rule FOR LUCKYDEX ONLY, not official TCG.
[LOCKED] Player and CPU should be able to attach up to this earned count without re-flipping during choices. Reuse preexisting deterministic coin RNG and log one four-result coin entry. Preserve Stage 9 and v07402 regression gates, update Stage 9 tests to test deterministic all-tails/one-head/four-head outcomes. Do not change Exavolt, Rip Claw or Wild Edge as part of this special case.
[CURRENT] This is a source-relative combined overlay on base APK 0.69.401; follow the previously tested source/ install and verify GitHub Actions APK build after push.
=== END THUNDER ROD HYBRID BALANCE OVERRIDE ===

=== GLOBAL HYBRID BASIC-ENERGY DECK-SEARCH COIN RULE — 23 NOVEMBER 2026 (v2.82) ===
[USER-DIRECTED BALANCE OVERRIDE] For Classic LuckyDex only, any PRINTED "search your deck for [a] Basic Energy" (including typed Basic Energy, and older descriptions adapted to "Basic Energy from separate supply") that takes a NEW Basic Energy from the finite Energy Zone must FLIP ONE FAIR COIN PER ENERGY REQUESTED. HEADS permits taking exactly ONE eligible actual Zone unit; TAILS adds/attaches nothing and leaves that unit in the Zone. A two-unit search gets two independent opportunities even if the first is tails; the maximum take remains subject to actual eligible supply. No Energy may be fabricated. Log the outcome with reason "Hybrid Basic Energy deck search".
[SCOPE] This applies when the deck search has been hybrid-converted to Zone retrieval, NOT when retrieving a physical Basic Energy card legitimately still in a draw deck. Do NOT add flips to retrieving from discard, hand attachments, ordinary turn Energy Zone attaches, Energy transfers, or "look at/reveal top N" conditional effects which are not deck searches (e.g. Max Elixir, Electric Generator, Serene Grace). Thunder Rod's separate user-approved FOUR coin-flips/reveal-top-four rule (v2.81) is unchanged and must never be double-gated by this global search rule.
[IMPLEMENTATION] Keep detection and the coin-gated ordered-Zone take in shared app/src/main/assets/tcgp-battle-effects.js via isPrintedBasicDeckSearch/searchAwareContext + neoSupplyTake/neoSupplyTakeAt. All new effects and legacy print-specific handlers accessing Energy Zone for a printed Basic Energy deck search must call the shared helper, not directly splice/shift energySupply. Preserve explicit choice of zone index and effect continuation after tails. Do not modify each printed card's official text or separate discard/other-energy mechanics.
[REGRESSION] Mandatory tests/v07404-basic-energy-search-coins.test.cjs is included in Gradle auditXY7ForApk and auditXY11ForApk as well as XY11 source/full preflight and its Gradle structural checklist. Tests cover heads/tails, repeated searches after tails, physical vs Zone conversion, unrelated attachments/discard retrieval, Base, EX Ruby, Diamond & Pearl, Black & White, Plasma Storm, Plasma Freeze, XY10 Rotom, and a real Classic trainer action pipeline. Preserve the older v07402 Rip Claw/Exavolt and v07403 CPU Wild Edge regression tests.
[BUILD] Combined overlay source-relative to existing source/ with no prepare script; versionName 0.69.401 and versionCode 7401 stay unchanged. Run the full GitHub/Termux catalogue build; the 98-test source-only preflight does not prove the published APK was built.
=== END GLOBAL HYBRID BASIC-ENERGY DECK-SEARCH COIN RULE ===

[FINDING] Printed hand-size attacks must respect effect.source=opponent. The shared Classic effect handCountDamage incorrectly used the attacking player's hand for every source. Reproduced with SM4 Mismagius Dark Arts: own hand 1 / defending hand 5 yielded 20, but printed attack requires 100. This also affected SM1 Krookodile False Accusation, whose base 20 plus 20 per opponent card needs the same engine-side correction. Use effect.source==='opponent' or effect.side==='opponent' to choose opposing hand; omit means own hand. Never change the source metadata on individual cards to hide this engine defect.

# v0.69.405 / Ultra Prism SM5 stage 1 — canonical print and gated-mechanics policy
- Ultra Prism (sm5) is the expansion immediately after Crimson Invasion (sm4). Official source: 178 prints (#1–#173 plus 119a/122a/125a/135a/153a); 237 attacks; 37 Abilities; 27 GX prints; six Prism Star prints; 36 Trainers; five special Energy prints. Canonical Git blob SHA: 66cbd13b706b789de3b8cce2f5c9fc95135a33b0 (upstream immutable commit 90f27d0e14cefe48ccb2b8922a7a356b2fcf0072).
- First install must run `sh source/scripts/prepare-sm5.sh` from the LuckyDex repository root AFTER unzipping the stage 1 patch under `source/`. This fetches source using the pinned SHA, writes immutable compact source+attestation, generates JS, then runs the SM5 preflight. `git add source && git commit && git push origin main` then gives CI a fully offline build. Never commit an SM5 generated catalogue without its SHA attestation.
- Never mark a Prism Star card playable until shared Lost Zone and one-per-name-across-prints deck limits are implemented and replay-tested for human and CPU. A one-copy metadata field alone is NOT sufficient.
- Never substitute all three possible energy types simultaneously for Unit Energy, or a free generic multicolour energy for Super Boost Energy. Unimplemented rules must gate whole prints until exact outcome tests pass.
- Cards share the SM1–SM4 strict reuse adapter (match printed text and Energy cost, not move name alone). Every generated descriptor is checked by the runtime validator, and any invalid effect gates that entire print. Do not conflate catalogue coverage with playable coverage.
- Run Termux commands with `&&` so a failed canonical download or audit never commits/pushes a broken partial expansion. Termux stage 1 install: `unzip -o /sdcard/Download/LuckyDex_patch_v0.69.405_SM5_ULTRA_PRISM_STAGE1.zip -d source && sh source/scripts/prepare-sm5.sh && git add source && git commit -m "LuckyDex v0.69.405 Ultra Prism SM5 stage 1" && git push origin main`.


# v0.69.406 / Ultra Prism SM5 stage 2: verified new attack/Trainer mechanics

- Add `scripts/sm5_stage2_verified.py` as the shared text-locked rules adapter for SM5. Each new attack uses its complete official printed effect text, not just its title. Each new Trainer compares its unique printed rule line. Never interpret matching titles as matching effects when their printed mechanics differ.
- Stage 2 contains 37 new attack-effect mappings and 10 Trainer/Stadium mappings, with a custom `sm5Rule:mars` so the opponent discards a random card only after you successfully drew cards. Include CPU-side outcomes and preserve physical discard identity.
- Energy searches using SM5 Charge, Synthesis, Searching Magnet and Volkner must use the existing virtual Basic Energy Zone, not fake Basic Energy cards in the physical draw deck. Mt. Coronet instead recovers ONLY Metal Energy actually in the discard pile or energyDiscard ledger. Test both side effects and conservation.
- Stage 2 mandatory outcome audit: `tests/v07427-ultra-prism-stage2-outcomes.test.cjs`, called from the Gradle auditSM5ForApk and the SM5 full preflight. Test exact Energy movement, target filters, Prize/hand mechanics where present, next-turn locks, and CPU parity.
- Preserve Prism Star gates (Lost Zone + 1-per-deck) and unsupported Unit/Super Boost Energy gates. Do not enable a print if even one Ability/attack/Trainer/Special Energy mechanic is unsupported. A 178-print catalogue is NOT a 178-playable catalogue.

# v0.69.407 / SM5 Ultra Prism Stage 3 — GX attacks and reusable Abilities

- All official SM5 Stage 3 rule mappings must be verified against exact printed attack, Ability and Trainer text, including GX parentheticals. Never claim a card fully playable unless ALL printed effects are implemented.

A failed attack requirement must stop all later effects in the sequence; never grant Moon Eclipse's attack immunity after an illegal declaration.

- Unidentified Fossil is a Pokémon-like Item with 60 HP. It can be placed on the Bench, never retreats, evolves by printed name, and can be voluntarily discarded using the existing Fossil action; preserve its actual reference UID when returning it to discard.

# v0.69.408 / SM5 Ultra Prism Stage 4 — physical Unit Energy, conditional Super Boost and Prism Lost Zone

- Ultra Prism **Unit Energy GFW** and **Unit Energy LPM** are physical Special Energy cards providing *one unit* chosen from their three printed types, or one Colorless; never three simultaneously. Track options per physical card. Mixed Energy costs require a whole-card assignment, and the same card cannot pay multiple typed cost symbols. This rule must apply to both CPU and player attack eligibility.
- **Super Boost Energy ◇**: one Colorless attached to a non-Stage-2 Pokemon; one any-type unit on Stage 2; four any-type units only when the owner has at least three Stage 2 Pokemon in play. It is a single physical Special Energy card. Crystal Beach reduces its four-unit state to one Colorless.
- **Prism Star**: maximum one physical copy of a given name across all alternate-art prints; if any Prism Star card would enter the physical discard pile, move that original card UID to the owner’s Lost Zone instead. Do not treat Prism Star as ordinary discard-recoverable. Do not enable other Prism Star Pokemon/Supporters until their effects are independently implemented.
- Four new text-locked passive Abilities (Intimidating Fang, Power Huddle, Flashing Head, Earthen Shield). Active-only, Bench-only, evolved opponent, Special Energy and Metal-only restrictions must be checked as appropriate.
- New mandatory `tests/v07429-ultra-prism-stage4-outcomes.test.cjs` battle and Energy assignment suite. The Stage 2 and Stage 3 suites remain mandatory. Never claim all SM5 prints playable while gated effects remain.
- `sh source/scripts/prepare-sm5.sh` fetches or reuses the SHA-locked official compact catalogue and runs the full release gate. Source-only local tests do **not** prove the GitHub APK build has passed.


# v0.69.409 / SM5 Ultra Prism Stage 5 — complete Prism Star battle rules

- Prism Star copies remain ONE per named card across alternate prints; all physical discard destinations normalise to the owning Lost Zone while preserving original UID. This includes the Cyrus ◇ Supporter after use.
- Stage 5 enables exactly five additional Prism Star prints (Giratina ◇ 58, Lunala ◇ 62, Darkrai ◇ 77, Solgaleo ◇ 89, Cyrus ◇ 120), along with Super Boost Energy ◇ 136 from Stage 4. Do not ungate any of these unless the exact official attack, Ability AND Trainer text matches the canonical SHA-pinned print. Do not unlock other unsupported SM5 cards with no-op descriptors.
- Chaotic Star and Nightmare Star trigger ONLY when their source is physically played from HAND to Bench, optionally attaching up to two matching BASIC Energy cards physically in hand, never from the fresh Energy Zone or discard. The trigger must remain usable by either player and choices must terminate.
- Full Moon Star and Radiant Star attach up to one matching discarded Basic Energy per opposing Pokemon actually in play; the owner chooses recipient Pokemon individually. Recover only from the real per-type discard ledger or physical Basic cards in discard, never the fresh Basic Energy supply.
- Abyssal Sleep uses two recovery flips and wakes ONLY on two heads; Crisis Dive discards two attached Energy cards; Corona Impact locks ALL attacks next own turn; Psystorm counts every attached Energy card across BOTH full boards with one count per physical Special Energy card.
- Cyrus ◇ can only be played while an OWN Active Water or Metal Pokemon is in play; the defending player selects two of their Benched Pokemon to preserve. The others and all physically attached cards are shuffled into the defending player's real deck. Validate pre-play legality so both player and CPU cannot spend an illegal Supporter.
- Stage 5 mandatory gameplay checks: `tests/v07430-ultra-prism-stage5-outcomes.test.cjs`. Preserve all Stage 2–4 and SM1–SM4 tests in the mandatory Gradle APK gate. Actual GitHub build green remains separately unverified until observed.
- RELEASE-GATE DRIFT PREVENTION (hotfix for v0.69.409): Whenever later stages implement previously gated cards, update all earlier catalogue and reuse test assertions in the SAME patch; never leave `battleEnabled === false` checks for formerly unsupported Prism Star or Special Energy. SM5 Stage 4 enables all 5 Special Energy prints (136,137,138,170,171), and Stage 5 enables all 6 Prism Star prints (58,62,77,89,120,136); they must be battleEnabled/deckEligible=true with proper effects, maxDeckCopies=1 for Prism Star, and Lost Zone rules still enforced. Tests v07424 and v07425 must assert the final cumulative contract, not the obsolete Stage 1 restrictions. Preflight --full must run after build-sm5.py on a canonical catalogue.

SM5 STAGE 7 / v0.69.411 REQUIREMENTS:

SM5 STAGE 10 / v0.69.414 RELEASE RULES:

SM5 STAGE 11 / v0.69.415 RELEASE RULES:

Rebuilding BW10 fixed all 27 tests in its three test files. An actual GitHub log is required to identify the specific failed job; local structural checks do not establish APK success.

=== v0.69.453 SM11 continuation: additional Prize and Tool-GX timing ===
- Keep the v451 indexed deck builder and performance tests intact. SM11 now enables 241/261 canonical prints; the current gap ledger identifies remaining blockers.
- A Tool-granted GX attack is a separately enumerated printed attack with a valid matching attack on its holder, its own typed cost, Tool suppression and the shared per-player GX restriction. It is not a Pokemon attack that Perfection may borrow from the discard. Both player and CPU use the same attack options.
- Injection-GX transfers one real opposing discard reference (or actual Basic Energy discard ledger unit, never the fresh Zone) to opposing face-down Prizes. Increase that player's victory threshold as well as their remaining pile; preserve the old prize-count fallback for historical/synthetic states. The targeted test verifies winning only after the added Prize is claimed.
- Weakness Guard Energy is attachment-scoped; check both ordinary Active and explicit Bench weakness paths. Do not confuse Energy suppression and Tool suppression.
- On-play Abilities use the engine's actual `benched` event with `fromHandOnly`, not a fictional `playedFromHand` event. Durable Blade requires opposing attack damage KO, retains only the top Pokemon, and preserves normal Prize awards and Energy discard.
- Super Growth searches the actual deck for each successive evolution; Victory Sign must use distinct Basic Energy types and the shared hybrid Zone search coin rule. Tropical Hour returns actual attached cards/Basic Energy to deck, not to the fresh Zone or discard. A start-of-next-turn draw may immediately move a shuffled card to hand; tests should check conservation across both zones.
- Optional and delayed effects need negative-path tests: clear Vision and Z-Crystals share GX lock; Dynamic Swing must not turn a zero-damage attack into damage; Drowsing tests both sleep recovery outcomes; Giant Bomb only reacts to >=180 damage, not counters, and expires after opponent's turn; Blue's Tactics draws at turn end, not on play.
- Remaining ledger includes attachment replacement/return rules (Recycle Energy, U-Turn Board), other timing/hand entry effects and quiz mechanics. Do not enable these with no-op or partial descriptors. Audit across historical attack/Ability sources, alternate arts, Trainer suppression, and both sides before calling the set complete.

v0.69.504 — universal fast packaged release verification
- Supersedes only the end-user invocation part of v495's preparation rule. The release author MUST still run the entire original latest-set prepare script on the final candidate before sealing a ZIP, plus `python3 -B tests/test_release_manifest.py`. Do not substitute source gates or fast hashing for full gameplay regression. CI/Gradle APK dependencies and all historical tests remain mandatory and unchanged.
- User command from repository root is now `sh source/scripts/prepare-release.sh` after `unzip -t` and `unzip -o`, before the usual `git add`, commit and push. The universal read-only SHA-256 verifier checks all cumulative patch members (except the manifest itself), historical generated TCG assets, pinned compact/source/coverage data, and global version consistency. No device-side generator, Node gameplay testing, Gradle, or network call. Failure exits nonzero; never advise recreating a manifest to silence mismatches. `--full` verifies the package and delegates to the original set-specific full chain. Original set-specific scripts remain usable for intentional local development.
- Before sealing each release, collect the cumulative patch list relative to source, including the verifier and current full script; package any covered historical assets that differ from the known base. Run `python3 -B scripts/release_manifest.py --create <newline-delimited-patch-list> --full-script prepare-<set>.sh` after all final file changes and full tests. Include `scripts/packaged_release_manifest.json` in the ZIP; no covered files may change after generation. Verify exact fast shell command, fresh ZIP overlay and CRC. Regenerate manifest every release. See docs/RELEASE_PREPARATION_WORKFLOW.md. SHA-256 is a consistency check, not authentication or proof that tests ran.
- Battle Styles remains 183/183. Six targeted independent audit checks cover split Kingdra recovery, damage-free KO, Tool Jammer/Rusted Shield maximum HP restoration. Broader independent audit of historical KO trigger ordering (including multi-recipient Exp. Share), copied attacks, and legacy Tool paths remains open. No gameplay engine changes in this release.
